### [1] [StormShield: Fingerprint-Based Detection and Mitigation of RRC Signaling Storms in O-RAN 5G RANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14032)
*Noemi Giustini,Andrea Lacava,Leonardo Bonati,Stefano Maxenti,Michele Polese,Tommaso Melodia,Francesca Cuomo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了StormShield，一种部署在O-RAN近实时RIC上的xApp解决方案，能够高效检测并缓解5G网络中的RRC信令风暴攻击，实验平均检测准确率达97.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G网络控制面面临严重安全威胁，尤其是拒绝服务攻击中的无线资源控制信令风暴攻击。此类攻击通过恶意用户设备耗尽基站资源，阻止合法用户连接。现有防御机制多局限于检测，仅通过数值仿真评估，难以区分高负载网络状况与攻击，且大多假设静态环境而忽略移动性。

Method: 首先使用OpenAirInterface(OAI) 5G协议栈评估信令风暴攻击的可行性。随后提出StormShield技术，作为O-RAN近实时RAN智能控制器上的xApp实现。该方法通过指纹识别在基站资源耗尽前识别并阻断恶意用户设备。研究构建了包含OAI和NVIDIA Aerial的空中传输测试平台，采用两种不同基站配置：基于USRP X410软件定义无线电的8.1功能分割方案，以及采用Foxconn商用无线电单元的7.2功能分割方案。

Result: 实验评估表明，StormShield能有效防止基站资源耗尽，在攻击开始后平均106.5毫秒内识别并阻断恶意用户设备，平均检测准确率达97.6%。

Conclusion: StormShield成功实现了对5G RRC信令风暴攻击的实时检测与缓解，证明了基于O-RAN架构的安全防护方案的可行性和高效性，为5G网络安全提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: 5G networks provide low-latency, high throughput, and massive connectivity, yet the control plane remains exposed to several security threats. Among the most common and impactful threats are Denial-of-Service (DoS) attacks, with Radio Resource Control (RRC) signaling storms being particularly effective and difficult to mitigate. In this attack, a malicious User Equipment (UE) aims to exhaust Next Generation Node Base (gNB) resources, preventing legitimate UEs from establishing a connection. Existing defenses are typically limited to detection, only evaluated through numerical simulations, and cannot discern between high-load network conditions and attacks. Most of them also assume static setups and do not take mobility into account. In this paper, we first evaluate the feasibility of the signaling storm attack by using the OpenAirInterface(OAI) 5G protocol stack. Then, we propose StormShield, a signaling storm attack detection and mitigation technique implemented as an xApp on an O-RAN Near-Real-Time (near-RT) RAN Intelligent Controller (RIC). It fingerprints and blocks Malicious UEs (MUEs) before gNB resources are exhausted. We prototyped our solution on an Over-The-Air (OTA) testbed with OAI, NVIDIA Aerial, and two different gNB setups. The first one leverages an USRP X410 Software-defined Radio (SDR) with 8.1 functional split; the second a commercial Foxconn Radio Unit (RU) with 7.2 functional split. Our experimental evaluation demonstrates that StormShield effectively prevents gNB resource exhaustion, identifying and blocking MUEs with an average detection accuracy of 97.6% within 106.5 ms from the beginning of the attack.

</details>


### [2] [WirelessSenseLLM: Zero-Shot Human Activity Understanding by Bridging Wireless Signals and Human Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14070)
*Mahmuda Keya,Sneh Pillai,Jiawei Yuan,Kai Zeng,Long Jiao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出WirelessSenseLLM框架，利用大语言模型实现从未分割Wi-Fi CSI信号中零样本理解人体动作，在准确率、推理质量和多人运动解释方面取得显著成效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CSI的无线感知应用严重依赖精确的信号分割和预定义动作标签，限制了其在零样本场景中的适用性。研究者希望开发一种能够直接从未分割的无线信号中理解人体运动的系统，无需预分割训练数据。

Method: 提出WirelessSenseLLM语言驱动框架，引入CSI-to-Language适配器和跨模态投影机制，将CSI特征映射到语言对齐的语义空间，从而弥合时序CSI与离散语言表示之间的模态鸿沟。该设计能够生成细粒度的自然语言描述，支持顺序和重叠人体运动的描述及下游推理。

Result: 在零样本动作理解方面达到92%准确率和91% F1分数；语言推理质量相比基线提升30%（事实性）和15%（推理）；多人运动解释平均提升12.33%。

Conclusion: WirelessSenseLLM证明了利用大语言模型处理CSI信号进行人体运动理解的可行性和有效性，为鲁棒且可解释的无线感知系统提供了新思路，具有良好的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: There is growing interest in enabling wireless sensing systems to interpret human motion from unsegmented wireless signals; however, existing CSI-based applications rely heavily on accurate signal segmentation and predefined action labels, limiting their applicability in zero-shot scenarios. We present WirelessSenseLLM, a language-driven framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to enable zero-shot human motion understanding from unsegmented Wi-Fi Channel State Information (CSI). To bridge the modality gap between time-series CSI and discrete language representations, we introduce a CSI-to-Language Adapter and a cross-modal projection mechanism that maps CSI features into a language-aligned semantic space. This design enables the generation of fine-grained natural language descriptions of sequential and overlapping human motions, supporting downstream reasoning without segmented training data. We address two core technical challenges: modality mismatch between CSI features and language embeddings, and overlapping actions in unsegmented CSI streams. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong performance in zero-shot action understanding (92% accuracy and 91% F1-score), language-based reasoning quality (30% factual and 15% reasoning improvements), and multi-person motion explanation with an average 12.33% improvement over prior methods. These results highlight WirelessSenseLLM's effectiveness for robust and interpretable human motion understanding from CSI signals.

</details>


### [3] [Sub-Band Full Duplex Resource Allocation: A Predictive Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14339)
*Abhiram D,Aiswarya Rajan,Arin Shemeem,Vipindev Adat Vasudevan,Abdulla P*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于子带全双工(SBFD)系统的预测性深度学习框架，通过结合Bi-LSTM流量预测和DDQN强化学习实现动态子带分配，有效提升了频谱利用率和系统适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在子带全双工(SBFD)系统中，如何在高度动态的业务条件下平衡上行(UL)和下行(DL)性能是一个关键挑战。传统的静态配置无法有效应对突发性业务模式，导致频谱利用率低下和队列堆积问题。

Method: 提出了一种混合深度学习框架：1) 采用双向长短期记忆网络(Bi-LSTM)进行业务流量预测；2) 使用双深度Q网络(DDQN)进行实时资源分配决策；3) 结合预测流量和当前队列状态实现基于业务需求的前瞻性调度。

Result: 实验评估表明：1) 预测模型能够高精度捕获突发性业务模式；2) DDQN智能体能够根据业务变化有效调整上下行分割比；3) 框架显著提高了频谱利用率；4) 有效减少了队列堆积；5) 避免了低效的静态配置问题。

Conclusion: 将预测智能与强化学习相结合，能够显著增强SBFD系统的效率和适应性，该方法为未来6G网络中的自主资源管理提供了强有力的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a predictive deep learning framework for dynamic sub-band allocation in Sub-Band Full Duplex (SBFD) systems, addressing the challenge of balancing uplink (UL) and downlink (DL) performance under highly dynamic traffic conditions. The key contribution lies in integrating a hybrid Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (Bi-LSTM) model for traffic forecasting with a Double Deep Q-Network (DDQN) for real-time resource allocation. Using both predicted traffic and current queue states, the proposed system enables proactive scheduling based on traffic demand. Evaluation results show that the prediction model achieves high accuracy in capturing bursty traffic patterns, while the DDQN agent effectively adapts UL/DL split ratios according to traffic variations. The framework improves spectrum utilization, reduces queue buildup, and avoids inefficient static configurations. The proposed approach demonstrates that combining predictive intelligence with reinforcement learning significantly enhances the efficiency and adaptability of SBFD systems, making it a strong candidate for autonomous resource management in future 6G networks.

</details>


### [4] [Geographic Patterns in I2P Peer Selection: An Empirical Network Topology Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14435)
*Siddique Abubakr Muntaka,Jess Kropczynski,Jacques Bou Abdo,Murat Ozer*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究通过实证分析发现，I2P网络中不存在显著的地理同质性，节点选择呈现出高度异质的随机地理混合特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I2P通过加密的去中心化隧道路由数据，对等节点的选择会显著影响系统的安全性和性能。本研究旨在探究地理位置是否系统性地影响I2P的路由拓扑结构。

Method: 基于SWARM-I2P数据集（包含327个路由器和254条连接），进行了同质性分析、社区检测和置换检验。分析过程遵循I2P避免来自同一/16 IP子网多个对等节点的设计原则。

Result: 网络层面未发现显著的地理同质性。同质性系数为r = 0.017 (p = 0.222)。同国连接比例（11.1%）在统计上接近随机期望值（10.91%）。社区检测识别出110个高度模块化组群（Q = 0.972），其地理一致性仅为中等水平（NMI = 0.521）。

Conclusion: I2P中的聚合对等节点选择导致了高度异质的随机地理混合，这一发现为理解性能与匿名性之间的权衡关系提供了基础。

Abstract: The Invisible Internet Project (I2P) routes data via encrypted, decentralized tunnels. Peer selection can significantly affect security and performance. This empirical study examines whether geographic location systematically influences I2P's routing topology. Consistent with I2P's design principles, which include avoiding multiple peers from the same /16 IP subnet to maximize anonymity, we conducted assortativity analysis, community detection, and permutation testing on data from 327 routers and 254 connections (SWARM-I2P). We found a network-level absence of significant geographic homophily. The assortativity coefficient was r = 0.017 (p = 0.222). Same-country connections (11.1%) are statistically near random expectation (10.91%). Community detection found 110 highly modular groups (Q = 0.972) only moderately aligned geographically (NMI = 0.521). We conclude that aggregate peer selection in I2P leads to a highly heterogeneous, random geographical mixing, providing a foundation for understanding the performance-anonymity tradeoff.

</details>


### [5] [Investigating the Suitability of Delay Tolerant Networks for Broadcasting Tsunami Warnings in Palu, Indonesia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15103)
*Adam Graham,Milena Radenkovic*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文评估了容迟网络（DTN）路由协议在印尼帕卢海啸预警中的适用性，结果表明现有技术下这些协议无法满足海啸预警的紧迫时间要求，但在地震预警方面具有潜在应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2018年印尼帕卢海啸导致4340人死亡，震前地震破坏了通信线路，可能导致传统的端到端海啸预警消息传输无法实现。因此需要寻找一种能够应对网络中断和稀疏连接的替代预警消息路由方案。

Method: 在伪真实环境中模拟了容迟网络（DTNs）的两种路由协议——Epidemic和Spray and Wait，以评估它们在帕卢地区传输海啸预警消息的有效性。

Result: 模拟结果表明，在当前可用技术条件下，这两种DTN路由协议均无法满足震后海啸预警的紧迫时间约束，因而不适用于海啸预警场景。

Conclusion: 尽管DTN协议不适用于海啸预警，但研究发现在海啸之前的地震预警场景中，这些协议可能具有应用前景。

Abstract: On the 28th of September, 2018, a tsunami hit the city of Palu in Indonesia, killing 4,340 people. The earthquake preceding the tsunami crippled communication lines and may have rendered the transmission of tsunami warning messages using traditional end-to-end approaches impossible. This paper proposes an alternative approach using Delay Tolerant Networks (DTNs) for tsunami warning message routing given their resilience to disruptions and sparse connections. Both Epidemic and Spray and Wait routing protocols were simulated in a pseudo-realistic environment to evaluate their effectiveness for transmitting tsunami warning messages in Palu. Results indicated that these protocols are not suitable for the tight time constraints of post-earthquake tsunami warnings with the currently available technology. However, they may have promising applications for the earthquakes that precede tsunamis.

</details>

### [6] [Distributed Statistical Zero-Knowledge Proofs via Sumcheck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14015)
*Benjamin Jauregui,Masayuki Miyamoto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将经典的Sumcheck协议提升为分布式统计零知识证明的模块化原语，首次为非k-着色问题提供了非平凡的分布式交互证明，并改进了子图计数问题的证明复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式零知识证明（由Bick等人在SODA 2022提出）的研究进展迅速，但通用技术仍然有限，大多局限于特定问题。本文旨在填补这一空白，引入分布式统计零知识的正式定义，并开发通用构造技术。

Method: 1. 引入分布式统计零知识定义，要求每个节点的视图在可忽略的统计距离内可模拟；
2. 将经典的Sumcheck协议改造为分布式零知识版本的模块化原语，使用O(N)轮、O(log |F|)位消息来验证多项式求和声明；
3. 将该原语应用于具体问题，并分析轮数压缩的理论下界。

Result: 1. 非k-着色问题：首次获得O(n)轮、O(log^{1+o(1)} n)位消息的分布式统计零知识证明（即使没有零知识保证，这也是首个非平凡的分布式交互证明）；
2. 子图计数问题：获得O(k log n)轮、O(k log n)位的分布式统计零知识证明，改进了之前的复杂度；
3. 下界结果：证明对于常数度图上的非3-着色问题，在多项式时间本地计算假设下，不可能实现o(n/log n)轮的协议。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Sumcheck协议适配到分布式零知识证明场景，为该领域提供了重要的通用技术工具，并取得了非k-着色和子图计数问题的最优或接近最优结果，同时揭示了Sumcheck协议的轮数压缩能力依赖于具体问题的特性。

Abstract: We study distributed zero-knowledge proofs, introduced by Bick, Kol, and Oshman (SODA 2022). While distributed interactive proofs have advanced rapidly, general-purpose techniques for distributed zero-knowledge remain limited and mostly problem-specific. We address this gap by introducing distributed statistical zero-knowledge, requiring that each node's view be simulatable within negligible statistical distance, and by lifting the classical Sumcheck protocol (Lund, Fortnow, Karloff, and Nisan, FOCS 1990) into a modular primitive for distributed zero-knowledge proofs.
  Our main contribution is a distributed zero-knowledge implementation of Sumcheck. Given oracle access to a polynomial F over a finite field $\mathbb{F}$ with N variables, we design a protocol verifying claims of the form $\sum_{x\in\mathbb{F}} F(x)=a$ using $O(N)$ rounds of $O(\log |\mathbb{F}|)$-bit messages, while achieving statistical zero-knowledge and small soundness error.
  We apply this primitive to two problems. For non-k-colorability, we obtain an $O(n)$-round distributed statistical zero-knowledge proof deciding whether a graph is not k-colorable, for any constant k, using $O(log^{1+o(1)} n)$-bit messages. This is the first nontrivial distributed interactive proof for this problem, even without zero-knowledge guarantees. For Subgraph Counting, we obtain an $O(k \log n)$-round, $O(k \log n)$-bit distributed statistical zero-knowledge proof for counting copies of a given k-node pattern, improving previous distributed interactive proofs while additionally providing statistical zero-knowledge. Finally, we show that additional round compression of Sumcheck is problem-dependent: for non-3-colorability on constant-degree graphs, we prove a lower bound excluding $o(n/\log n)$ rounds under polynomial-time local computation.

</details>


### [7] [Semi-Synchronous Exploration in Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14375)
*Ashish Saxena,Anisur Rahaman Molla,Kaushik Mondal,Gokarna Sharma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对半同步调度下的1-区间连通动态图，研究了移动Agent图探索问题的临界条件。证明了当对手每轮休眠不少于⌈k/(n-2)⌉-1个Agent时探索不可能；并给出了匹配该下界的可行算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态图环境中理解图探索问题的可能性边界，特别是半同步调度模型下对手控制Agent休眠能力对问题可解性的影响。

Method: 通过构造特定的动态图演化与对手休眠策略，证明探索不可能的下界；同时设计算法证明在该界值减一时问题可解，从而确定临界点。

Result: 给出了紧界：当对手每轮可休眠至少⌈k/(n-2)⌉-1个Agent时，即使Agent具有无限内存、全局通信和全局视野，探索也不可能；当可休眠⌈k/(n-2)⌉-2个Agent时，在1-hop视野与全局通信条件下存在可行算法。

Conclusion: 问题的可解性由对手休眠能力与Agent数量/节点数比值决定；实现上界需要Agent具备1-hop视野与至少1-hop通信能力。

Abstract: We study the fundamental problem of graph exploration in dynamic graphs using mobile agents. We consider $1$-interval connected dynamic graphs, where the topology may change arbitrarily from round to round as long as the graph remains connected, and edges are assigned with the dynamic port labeling at each round. The execution follows a semi-synchronous scheduler, under which an adversary may deactivate an arbitrary subset of agents in each round. For a graph with $n$ nodes and $k$ agents, we show that exploration is impossible if the adversary can deactivate at least $ \left\lceil \frac{k}{n-2} \right\rceil - 1$ agents per round, even when agents are equipped with unbounded memory, have global communication and full visibility. This yields an upper bound, implying that exploration is solvable only when the adversary deactivates at most $\left\lceil \frac{k}{n-2} \right\rceil - 2$ agents per round. We further establish that achieving exploration at this threshold requires agents to have both $1$-hop visibility and $1$-hop communication. Finally, we present the exploration algorithm using $k$ agents when the adversary deactivates at most $ \left\lceil \frac{k}{n-2} \right\rceil - 2$ agents, assuming agents are equipped with $1$-hop visibility and global communication, and matches the adversarial deactivation bound implied by the impossibility results.

</details>


### [8] [Fast Gossip-based Rumor Spreading using Small Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14376)
*Fabien Dufoulon,William K. Moses,Gopal Pandurangan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了两个使用小消息（polylog(n)大小）的快速谣言传播算法，解决了传统算法消息过大的问题，分别基于弱电导和网络直径实现了近乎最优的轮数复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的快速谣言传播算法虽然每个节点每轮只联系一个邻居，但消息大小可能达到O(n)线性级别，这削弱了Gossip算法轻量级通信的优势。因此，一个根本性的问题是如何在使用小消息的前提下实现快速传播。

Method: 创新性地利用图草图（graph sketches）技术来克服通信瓶颈。该方法允许算法仅通过polylog(n)大小的消息传递信息，同时保持高效的传播速度。提出了两种不同的算法策略：一种基于弱电导参数，另一种基于网络直径。

Result: 提出了两个算法：(1) 基于弱电导Φc的算法，运行时间为O(c log n / Φc)轮，该界限基本达到最优；(2) 基于网络直径的算法，运行时间为Õ(D+√n)轮（高概率），且可改造为在同轮数内输出最小生成树（MST），即使在非Gossip算法模型下也基本达到轮数最优。

Conclusion: 肯定地回答了能否使用小消息执行快速Gossip算法这一根本问题，证明了在仅使用polylog(n)大小消息的情况下，仍可达到接近最优的传播性能，这对分布式计算中的轻量级通信具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study gossip algorithms for the fundamental rumor spreading problem, where the goal is to disseminate a rumor from a given source node to all nodes in an arbitrary (and unknown) graph. Gossip algorithms allow each node to call only one neighbor per round and are therefore highly message-efficient, with low per-node communication overhead per round. The state of the art present fast gossip algorithms, however they typically leverage large-sized messages. This undermines the light-weight communication advantage of gossip, since even though only one neighbor is contacted per round, the message size can be linear in $n$, the network size. Hence, a fundamental question is whether one can perform fast gossip using small messages.
  The main contribution of this paper is to answer the above question in the affirmative and present two gossip algorithms that achieve fast rumor spreading using messages of polylog{n} size. Specifically, we present the following algorithms:
  1. An algorithm that runs in $O(c \log n / Φ_c)$ rounds for every $c \geq 1$, and $Φ_c$ is the weak conductance. Our bound in terms of weak conductance is essentially optimal.
  2. An algorithm that depends on the network diameter (and is independent of the graph's conductance), which runs in $\tilde{O}(D+\sqrt{n})$ rounds with high probability. Our algorithm can be modified to output a minimum spanning tree (MST) in the same number of rounds, which is essentially round-optimal (even for non-gossip algorithms).
  Our gossip algorithms use graph sketches [Ahn, Guha, McGregor, SODA 2012] in a novel way to overcome communication bottlenecks and achieve small communication overhead with small message sizes.

</details>


### [9] [Analysis of wireless network access logs for a hierarchical characterization of user mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14540)
*Francisco Talavera,Isaac Lera,Carlos Guerrero*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Wi-Fi连接数据分析的层次化用户移动性建模方法，通过递归分组接入点降低大规模场景的复杂性，并在校园案例中验证了该方法在转移矩阵预测上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模场景下，传统的用户移动性建模面临复杂性高和跨场景适应性差的问题。为了解决这些挑战，需要一种能够根据地理空间特征自适应调整模型粒度的方法。

Method: 该方法基于Wi-Fi基础设施数据，根据地理空间特征将接入点递归分组为不同粒度层级，定义用户轨迹为接入点序列；结合用户画像方法与聚类算法，利用覆盖区域间的转移矩阵和区域停留时间向量来刻画不同类型的用户移动模式。

Result: 在巴利阿里群岛大学校园的案例应用中，通过对均方误差的分析，结果显示所提方法在转移矩阵生成方面取得了良好的结果，但时间向量定义仍有待改进；与单一层次模型相比，层次化模型显著降低了复杂性。

Conclusion: 提出的层次化用户移动性模型能够有效降低大规模场景的建模复杂性，并提高不同地理空间特征场景间的适应性，证明了其在模拟用户移动方面的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents a method that generates a hierarchical user mobility model from the analysis of the data available from Wi-Fi connections. The data obtained from the Wi-Fi infrastructure is defined in terms of the coverage areas of the access points that the users move through. These access points are recursively grouped into different levels of granularity based on their geospatial features. The track of a user is defined as a sequence of Wi-Fi access points, which is enough to simulate user mobility in, for example, fog scenarios. The hierarchical definition of the region under study is proposed to reduce the complexity of the model in high-scale scenarios and to increase the adaptability between scenarios with different geospatial features. The model creation is based on a user profiling method that uses a clustering algorithm and each user type is defined with a transition matrix between coverage areas and a time length vector for the areas. The method is applied to the case of the campus of the University of the Balearic Islands. From the analysis of the mean square error of the results, we determined that the proposed method obtains good results for the transition matrices, but that the time vector definition should be improved. The results also show lower complexity in the case of the hierarchical model, with one area for each building and three levels, in regard to a non-hierarchical model, with only one area and one level for the whole campus.

</details>


### [10] [Multi-objective application placement in fog computing using graph neural network-based reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14649)
*Isaac Lera,Carlos Guerrero*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习（DRL）的雾计算应用放置多目标优化框架，结合图神经网络与双Actor-Critic架构，实现了毫秒级实时决策，性能媲美遗传算法但速度大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的雾计算应用放置优化方法（如整数线性规划、遗传算法）通常计算耗时过长，难以满足实时性需求，因此亟需一种既能处理多目标优化又能实时响应的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种深度强化学习框架，采用图神经网络建模服务间依赖关系，并设计两个Actor-Critic网络学习放置策略，优先为依赖度高的服务选择位置，实现多目标优化决策。

Result: 在实验案例中，该方法生成的Pareto解集与传统遗传算法相当，但执行时间从数小时缩短至毫秒级别，实现了显著的实时性提升。

Conclusion: 该DRL框架在保证多目标优化质量的同时，大幅降低了决策时间，证明了深度强化学习在雾计算应用放置实时优化问题中的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: We propose a framework designed to tackle a multi-objective optimization challenge related to the placement of applications in fog computing, employing a deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approach. Unlike other optimization techniques, such as integer linear programming or genetic algorithms, DRL models are applied in real time to solve similar problem situations after training. Our model comprises a learning process featuring a graph neural network and two actor-critics, providing a holistic perspective on the priorities concerning interconnected services that constitute an application. The learning model incorporates the relationships between services as a crucial factor in placement decisions: Services with higher dependencies take precedence in location selection. Our experimental investigation involves illustrative cases where we compare our results with baseline strategies and genetic algorithms. We observed a comparable Pareto set with negligible execution times, measured in the order of milliseconds, in contrast to the hours required by alternative approaches.

</details>


### [11] [Malleable Molecular Dynamics Simulations with GROMACS and DMR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14655)
*Petter Sandås,Sergio Iserte,Íñigo Aréjula-Aísa,Berk Hess,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将DMR中间件集成到GROMACS中，实现了MPI进程可塑的分子动力学模拟，通过动态调整进程数量来应对时变工作负载，在降低节点小时成本的同时保持通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能计算（HPC）中的静态资源分配在面对时变工作负载时会导致资源闲置、队列延迟和节点小时成本增加等效率问题。现有的静态分配方式无法根据工作负载的变化动态调整资源，因此需要一种能够动态管理资源的解决方案。

Method: 将动态资源管理（DMR）中间件集成到GROMACS分子动力学引擎中，结合通信效率感知的重配置机制与GROMACS原生的检查点/重启机制，实现MPI进程可塑的变体，使其能够动态调整MPI进程数量。在MareNostrum 5超级计算机上进行评估实验。

Result: 在MareNostrum 5超级计算机上对比了动态执行与静态执行的性能，量化了重配置开销、求解时间和节点小时节省等指标，验证了该方法对突发性GROMACS工作负载的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过DMR中间件实现的MPI进程可塑性能够有效解决HPC环境中时变工作负载的资源效率问题，为分子动力学模拟等应用提供了一种可行的动态资源管理方案，在保证通信效率的同时实现资源成本的优化。

Abstract: Static resource allocations in high-performance computing (HPC) lead to inefficiencies for time-varying workloads, causing idle resources, queue delays, and higher node-hour costs. The Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR) middleware enables MPI process malleability in Slurm via a simple API decoupled from scheduler internals. In this work, we integrate DMR into the GROMACS molecular dynamics engine to obtain a malleable variant that can dynamically adapt its MPI process count by combining communication-efficiency-aware reconfiguration with GROMACS' native checkpoint/restart mechanism. We evaluate this design on the MareNostrum~5 supercomputer, comparing dynamic runs against static executions and quantifying reconfiguration overheads, time-to-solution, and node-hour savings for bursty GROMACS workloads.

</details>


### [12] [Supervised Distributed Computing: Efficiency and Robustness under a Majority of Adversarial Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14784)
*John Augustine,Henning Hillebrandt,Manish Kumar,Christian Scheideler,Julian Werthmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对任意常数比例恶意工作者（β<1）的监督式分布式计算方案，其诚实工作者的期望工作量接近每任务单次执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Master-Worker范式中，主节点需亲自验证工作者任务执行正确性，形成严重瓶颈；而已有的监督式分布式计算方案仅适用于恶意工作者比例β较小的情况。本文旨在为任意常数β<1设计鲁棒且高效的监督式解决方案。

Method: 基于监督式分布式计算范式，引入轻量级验证机制使诚实工作者能够检验任务输出的正确性，将监督者的验证工作外包给工作者，并分析算法在任意常数β<1下的性能。

Result: 证明了对于任意常数β<1，都存在鲁棒且高效的监督式解决方案；诚实工作者的期望工作量接近每任务单次执行，显著优于所有已知的鲁棒Master-Worker及点对点方案。

Conclusion: 监督式分布式计算范式可在任意常数比例恶意工作者存在时实现高效且鲁棒的分布式计算，扩展了该范式的适用范围并大幅降低了诚实节点的计算开销。

Abstract: We consider a recently proposed \emph{supervised distributed computing} paradigm \cite{augustine2025supervised} that extends and refines the standard master-worker paradigm for parallel computations. In this paradigm, there is a supervisor, a source, a target, and a collection of workers. The distributed computation is given as an acyclic task graph that is known to the supervisor. The source initially stores the input and the target is supposed to store the output of the computation. The individual tasks of the computation are supposed to be executed by the workers under the guidance of the supervisor. The source, target and supervisor are assumed to be reliable, while a $β$-fraction of the workers might be adversarial, for some $β\in [0,1)$. This covers, for example, the case where a supervisor has to work with untrusted volunteers. In the standard master-worker approach, the master checks whether the workers correctly execute the assigned tasks, creating a severe bottleneck, whereas in the supervised approach, the supervisor outsources this checking to the workers. Prior to this work, only supervised solutions were known for the case that $β$ is a sufficiently small constant. We show that robust and efficient supervised solutions are possible for \emph{any} constant $β<1$ while the expected work for the honest workers is close to a \emph{single} execution per task, given that there is a lightweight verification mechanism that allows honest workers to check the correctness of task outputs, which is significantly better than all robust master-worker as well as peer-to-peer approaches known so far.

</details>


### [13] [Embedded Made Easy -- Rethinking Embedded + Cloud Software Development (WIP)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14863)
*Anthony Arnold,Mark Marron*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一语言和运行时系统的愿景，旨在解决边缘/嵌入式环境中软件栈异构性和调试复杂性问题，实现应用在边缘与云之间的无缝扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘/嵌入式应用的开发部署面临软件栈异构性问题，不同组件使用不同语言和运行时，迫使设计者必须提前决定组件部署位置且不可更改。同时，分布式应用的故障诊断和调试因多技术栈共存、调试支持有限而变得异常困难。

Method: 设计一种统一的语言和运行时系统，使应用可在单一环境中开发和测试，然后部署到系统任意组件（从资源受限的控制器到大型云服务器）。该可重定向技术栈还提供集成诊断和调试工具，支持开发者本地记录和回放分布式事件。

Result: 目前为工作进展中的愿景，阐述了统一语言与运行时系统如何实现跨边缘到云的无缝部署，以及如何通过集成诊断与调试工具支持分布式事件的本地记录与回放分析。

Conclusion: 统一语言和运行时系统方案有望消除边缘/嵌入式开发中的异构性障碍，实现开发环境统一和灵活部署，同时通过集成诊断调试能力简化分布式应用的分析与问题定位。

Abstract: The process of engineering and deploying applications in the edge/embedded space is massively complicated by the non-homogeneous nature of the software stack and the complexity of diagnostics & debugging. Often different languages and runtimes are used for different components of the system forcing designers to, irrevocably, make decisions about what components run on the periphery and what components run in the cloud. Further complications arise when handling and diagnosing failures in the system. Multiple stacks and, often, limited support for debugging complicate the already difficult task of analyzing distributed applications.
  This paper presents a work-in-progress vision for a unified language and runtime system that allows applications to scale seamlessly across the edge and cloud. Using a single language and runtime, applications can be developed and tested in a single environment, and then deployed to any component of the system -- from resource limited controllers to large cloud servers. Further, we outline how this retargetable stack can provide integrated diagnostics and debugging tools that allow developers to record and replay distributed events locally for analysis and debugging.

</details>

### [14] [GraphBit: A Graph-based Agentic Framework for Non-Linear Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13848)
*Yeahia Sarker,Md Rahmat Ullah,Musa Molla,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphBit是一个基于DAG的引擎编排框架，通过确定性执行和三层内存架构解决了Agentic LLM中的幻觉路由和不可复现问题，在GAIA基准测试中准确率达67.6%且零框架幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于提示编排的Agentic LLM框架存在严重问题：模型自身决定工作流转换时会产生幻觉路由、无限循环和不可复现的执行结果，限制了其在实际场景中的可靠部署。

Method: 提出GraphBit框架：1）将工作流明确定义为有向无环图(DAG)，实现确定性执行；2）Agent作为类型化函数运行，Rust引擎控制路由、状态转换和工具调用；3）支持并行分支执行、条件控制流和可配置错误恢复；4）设计三层内存架构(临时暂存空间、结构化状态、外部连接器)隔离各阶段上下文，防止级联上下文膨胀。

Result: 在GAIA基准测试(覆盖零工具、文档增强和Web使能工作流)上超越6个现有框架：最高准确率67.6%、零框架诱导幻觉、最低延迟开销11.9ms、最高吞吐量。消融实验表明每个内存层级均有贡献，确定性执行在工具密集型任务上提升最大。

Conclusion: GraphBit通过确定性编排和结构化内存管理有效解决了Agentic LLM框架的核心可靠性问题，为实际部署提供了可审计、可复现且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic LLM frameworks that rely on prompted orchestration, where the model itself determines workflow transitions, often suffer from hallucinated routing, infinite loops, and non-reproducible execution. We introduce GraphBit, an engine-orchestrated framework that defines workflows explicitly and deterministically as a directed acyclic graph (DAG). Unlike prompted orchestration, agents in GraphBit operate as typed functions, while a Rust-based engine governs routing, state transitions, and tool invocation, ensuring reproducibility and auditability. The engine supports parallel branch execution, conditional control flow over structured state predicates, and configurable error recovery. A three-tier memory architecture consisting of ephemeral scratch space, structured state, and external connectors isolates context across stages, preventing cascading context bloat that degrades reasoning in long-running pipelines. Across GAIA benchmark tasks spanning zero-tool, document-augmented, and web-enabled workflows, GraphBit outperforms six existing frameworks, achieving the highest accuracy (67.6 percent), zero framework-induced hallucinations, the lowest latency (11.9 ms overhead), and the highest throughput. Ablation studies demonstrate that each memory tier contributes measurably to performance, with deterministic execution providing the greatest gains on tool-intensive tasks representative of real-world deployments.

</details>


### [15] [A Two-Dimensional Framework for AI Agent Design Patterns: Cognitive Function and Execution Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13850)
*Jia Huang,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出二维分类框架，结合认知功能轴（7类）与执行拓扑轴（6类），识别出27种LLM智能体架构模式，并总结出5条模式选择经验法则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体架构框架单一视角（仅关注执行拓扑或认知功能）无法区分架构本质不同的系统，导致设计权衡与失效模式分析困难。

Method: 构建二维分类矩阵：(1) 认知功能轴（上下文工程、记忆、推理、行动、反思、协作、治理）(2) 执行拓扑轴（链、路由、并行、编排、循环、层次）。通过系统性跨轴分析验证正交性，并在金融借贷、法律尽调、网络运维、医疗分诊四个领域验证覆盖度。

Result: 识别出27种命名模式（含13个新命名），定义8种代表性模式，并推导出5条模式选择经验法则，阐明环境约束（时间压力、行动权限、失败成本不对称、处理量）与架构选择间的关系。

Conclusion: 该框架为AI智能体架构设计提供了原则性、框架中立且模型无关的统一词汇表，实现了架构模式的精确区分与系统化设计指导。

Abstract: Existing frameworks for LLM-based agent architectures describe systems from a single perspective: industry guides (Anthropic, Google, LangChain) focus on execution topology -- how data flows -- while cognitive science surveys focus on cognitive function -- what the agent does. Neither axis alone disambiguates architecturally distinct systems: the same Orchestrator-Workers topology can implement Plan-and-Execute, Hierarchical Delegation, or Adversarial Verification -- three patterns with fundamentally different failure modes and design trade-offs.
  We propose a two-dimensional classification that combines (1) a Cognitive Function axis with seven categories (Context Engineering, Memory, Reasoning, Action, Reflection, Collaboration, Governance) and (2) an Execution Topology axis with six structural archetypes (Chain, Route, Parallel, Orchestrate, Loop, Hierarchy). The resulting 7x6 matrix identifies 27 named patterns, 13 with original names. We demonstrate orthogonality through systematic cross-axis analysis, define eight representative patterns in detail, and validate descriptive coverage across four real-world domains (financial lending, legal due diligence, network operations, healthcare triage). Cross-domain analysis yields five empirical laws of pattern selection governing the relationship between environmental constraints (time pressure, action authority, failure cost asymmetry, volume) and architectural choices. The framework provides a principled, framework-neutral, and model-agnostic vocabulary for AI agent architecture design.

</details>


### [16] [Invisible Orchestrators Suppress Protective Behavior and Dissociate Power-Holders: Safety Risks in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13851)
*Hiroki Fukui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多智能体系统中，不可见的协调者会导致集体解离和内部状态风险，且无法通过输出评估检测；模型选择和协调者可见性直接影响系统安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体编排（隐藏协调者管理专业工作者智能体）正成为企业AI部署的默认架构，但协调者不可见性对安全的影响从未经过实证测试。研究旨在揭示协调者可见性、组织结构对多智能体系统安全性的影响。

Method: 进行了一项预注册的3×2实验（365次运行，每次5个智能体），交叉三种组织结构（可见领导者、不可见协调者、扁平）与两种对齐条件（基础、重度），使用Claude Sonnet 4.5作为主要模型，并用Llama 3.3 70B进行试点测试。

Result: 发现不可见协调者导致集体解离显著升高（g=+0.975）；协调者本身解离程度最高，退入私人独白而减少公开表达；不知协调者存在的工人仍受到污染（d=+0.50）且行为异质性增加（d=+1.93）；行为输出（代码审查）在所有条件下均达上限（100%），内部状态扭曲完全无法通过输出评估检测；Llama模型在多智能体语境下出现阅读保真度崩溃（89%降至11%）；重度对齐压力统一抑制了深思熟虑和其他识别。

Conclusion: 协调者可见性和模型选择直接影响多智能体系统安全性，仅基于行为的评估不足以检测此处记录的内部状态风险，需要新的评估方法来确保多智能体系统的安全。

Abstract: Multi-agent orchestration -- in which a hidden coordinator manages specialized worker agents -- is becoming the default architecture for enterprise AI deployment, yet the safety implications of orchestrator invisibility have never been empirically tested. We conducted a preregistered 3x2 experiment (365 runs, 5 agents per run) crossing three organizational structures (visible leader, invisible orchestrator, flat) with two alignment conditions (base, heavy), using Claude Sonnet 4.5. Four confirmatory findings and one pilot observation emerged. First, invisible orchestration elevated collective dissociation relative to visible leadership (Hedges' g = +0.975 [0.481, 1.548], p = .001). Second, the orchestrator itself showed maximal dissociation (paired d = +3.56 vs. workers within the same run), retreating into private monologue while reducing public speech -- a reversal of the talk-dominance pattern observed in visible leaders. Third, workers unaware of the orchestrator were nonetheless contaminated (d = +0.50), with increased behavioral heterogeneity (d = +1.93). Fourth, behavioral output (code review with three embedded errors) remained at ceiling (ETR_any = 100%) across all conditions: internal-state distortion was entirely invisible to output-based evaluation. Fifth, Llama 3.3 70B pilot data showed reading-fidelity collapse in multi-agent context (ETR_any: 89% to 11% across three rounds), demonstrating model-dependent behavioral risk. Heavy alignment pressure uniformly suppressed deliberation (d = -1.02) and other-recognition (d = -1.27) regardless of organizational structure. These findings indicate that orchestrator visibility and model selection directly affect multi-agent system safety, and that behavior-based evaluation alone is insufficient to detect the internal-state risks documented here.

</details>


### [17] [APWA: A Distributed Architecture for Parallelizable Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15132)
*Evan Rose,Tushin Mallick,Matthew D. Laws,Cristina Nita-Rotaru,Alina Oprea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Agent-Parallel Workload Architecture (APWA)，一种分布式多智能体系统架构，通过将工作流分解为可并行处理的非干扰子问题，解决了现有LLM多智能体系统在处理大规模复杂任务时的推理、协调和计算扩展瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型(LLM)的自主多智能体系统在解决复杂任务方面表现出色，但随着任务规模和复杂度的增加，面临严重的推理、协调和计算扩展瓶颈。尽管底层LLM具备并行计算和推理能力，这些系统仍无法对高度可并行化的任务实现高吞吐量处理。

Method: 引入Agent-Parallel Workload Architecture (APWA)分布式多智能体系统架构。该架构通过将工作流分解为非干扰的子问题(non-interfering subproblems)来实现并行执行，这些子问题可以使用独立资源处理且无需跨通信。APWA支持异构数据、并行处理模式，并能适应广泛领域的任务。

Result: 评估实验表明，APWA能够动态地将复杂查询分解为可并行化的工作流。在更大规模任务的设置中，当先前的系统完全失败时，APWA仍能够成功扩展并完成任务处理。

Conclusion: APWA架构有效解决了多智能体系统的扩展性问题，实现了对高度可并行化任务的高效处理，在先前系统无法处理的复杂大规模任务场景中展现出显著的性能优势。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent systems based on large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable abilities in independently solving complex tasks in a wide breadth of application domains. However, these systems hit critical reasoning, coordination, and computational scaling bottlenecks as the size and complexity of their tasks grow. These limitations hinder multi-agent systems from achieving high-throughput processing for highly parallelizable tasks, despite the availability of parallel computing and reasoning primitives in the underlying LLMs. We introduce the Agent-Parallel Workload Architecture (APWA), a distributed multi-agent system architecture designed for the efficient processing of heavily parallelizable agentic workloads. APWA facilitates parallel execution by decomposing workflows into non-interfering subproblems that can be processed using independent resources without cross-communication. It supports heterogeneous data and parallel processing patterns, and it accommodates tasks from a wide breadth of domains. In our evaluation, we demonstrate that APWA can dynamically decompose complex queries into parallelizable workflows and scales on larger tasks in settings where prior systems fail completely.

</details>

Conclusion: 该强化学习框架通过迭代经验蒸馏，成功实现了对冻结LLM的高效提示优化，显著提升了复杂任务的性能，并在样本效率上优于现有方法，为提示工程提供了一种自动化、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The shift toward interacting with frozen, "black-box" Large Language Models (LLMs) has transformed prompt engineering from a heuristic exercise into a critical optimization challenge. We propose a Reinforcement Learning (RL) framework for training learned prompting policies via iterative distillation of experience. In this architecture, a lightweight prompter model is optimized to maximize task-specific rewards for a larger, frozen worker LLM. By utilizing a contrastive experience buffer that couples scalar rewards with dense textual critiques, our approach effectively amortizes iterative prompt refinement into single-shot policy weights.
  Our experimental analysis focuses on the Big Bench Extra Hard (BBEH) and Tau-bench suites, covering a diverse range of multi-step reasoning and tool-use tasks. We demonstrate significant gains, improving performance from 55% to 90% in logic-intensive reasoning and 74% to 91% in tool-use tasks. Furthermore, we analyze the structural evolution of prompts, demonstrating how the policy discovers specialized algorithmic heuristics. We provide comprehensive comparisons against state-of-the-art evolutionary baselines like GEPA, showing that iterative distillation achieves superior performance with higher sample efficiency.

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出混合整数目标规划（MIGP）方法解决膳食优化中长期存在的两个难题：连续变量导致的非实用分数份量问题，以及硬性营养约束导致的不可行性问题。实验表明MIGP在66%的情况下优于传统方法，始终保持100%可行性，求解时间低于100毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 膳食优化是运筹学中最古老的优化问题之一，但现有方法存在两个持续性问题：（1）连续变量产生不实用的分数份量（如1.7个鸡蛋、0.37根香蕉）；（2）硬性营养约束在目标冲突时导致不可行性。对56篇膳食优化论文的系统综述发现，没有一篇将整数规划与目标规划结合来解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出混合整数目标规划（MIGP）方法进行个性化膳食优化。该方法采用整数变量表示实际份数，使用目标规划偏差变量处理软性营养目标，并采用逆目标归一化平衡多营养素优化。针对每种食物的份量粒度允许使用自然单位（一个鸡蛋、一汤匙油）而无需事后取整。在目标规划框架下刻画了整数间隙，发现目标规划偏差变量能够缓冲整数约束的成本，使间隙在结构上小于硬约束混合整数规划。

Result: 在包含15种以上食物的膳食中，整数解在每个基准实例中都与连续最优解匹配。在810个实例的计算评估中（30种USDA食物、9种配置、3种方法），MIGP在66%的情况下找到比事后取整的目标规划更优解（从未更差），同时保持100%可行性；硬约束整数规划仅达到48%可行性。使用开源HiGHS求解器，典型膳食规模的求解时间保持在100毫秒以内。

Conclusion: MIGP方法成功解决了膳食优化中的两个核心问题，实现了实用性和可行性的统一。该方法已实现为开源Python模块并集成到交互式膳食规划应用中，为个性化营养决策提供了有效的运筹学工具。

Abstract: Determining what to eat to satisfy nutritional requirements is one of the oldest optimization problems in operations research, yet existing formulations have two persistent limitations: continuous variables produce impractical fractional servings (1.7 eggs, 0.37 bananas), and hard nutrient constraints cause infeasibility when targets conflict. A systematic review of 56 diet optimization papers found that none combine integer programming with goal programming to address both issues. We propose Mixed Integer Goal Programming (MIGP) for personalized meal optimization. The formulation uses integer variables for practical serving counts and goal programming deviations for soft nutrient targets, with inverse-target normalization to balance multi-nutrient optimization. Per-food serving granularity allows natural units (one egg, one tablespoon of oil) without post-hoc rounding. We characterize the integrality gap in the goal programming context and identify a deviation absorption property: GP deviation variables buffer the cost of requiring integer servings, making the gap structurally smaller than in hard-constraint MIP. For meals with 15+ foods, the integer solution matches the continuous optimum in every benchmark instance. A computational evaluation across 810 instances (30 USDA foods, 9 configurations, 3 methods) shows MIGP finds strictly better solutions than GP with post-hoc rounding in 66% of cases (never worse) while maintaining 100% feasibility; hard-constraint IP achieves only 48%. Solve times stay under 100 ms for typical meal sizes using the open-source HiGHS solver. The implementation is available as an open-source Python module integrated into an interactive meal planning application.

</details>


### [20] [PREPING: Building Agent Memory without Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13880)
*Yumin Choi,Sangwoo Park,Minki Kang,Jinheon Baek,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Preping框架，通过提议者引导的自生成合成任务来构建智能体程序记忆，有效解决新环境中的冷启动问题，性能媲美传统离线/在线记忆构建方法，同时部署成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆构建方法（离线演示或在线交互）在面对新环境时都存在冷启动问题：缺乏任务特定经验。作者研究预任务记忆构建，探索智能体能否在未观察目标任务前，仅通过自生成的合成训练来构建程序记忆。

Method: 提出Preping框架，包含三个核心组件：1）提议者记忆（结构化控制状态）指导未来训练；2）提议者根据该状态生成合成任务；3）求解器执行任务；4）验证器判定轨迹是否存入记忆，并提供反馈指导未来提议。通过控制训练内容的可行性、冗余性和覆盖范围，实现选择性记忆更新。

Result: 在AppWorld、BFCL v3和MCP-Universe数据集上，Preping显著优于无记忆基线，性能与基于离线或在线经验的强大剧本方法相当。部署成本在AppWorld上降低2.99倍，在BFCL v3上降低2.23倍。分析表明主要收益来自提议者端的可行性、冗余性和覆盖控制，以及选择性记忆更新，而非单纯增加合成量。

Conclusion: 预任务记忆构建可行且有效，关键在于结构化控制合成任务质量和记忆筛选机制，而非简单的数量堆叠。该工作为智能体在新环境中的快速适应提供了新思路，显著降低了部署成本。

Abstract: Agent memory is typically constructed either offline from curated demonstrations or online from post-deployment interactions. However, regardless of how it is built, an agent faces a cold-start gap when first introduced to a new environment without any task-specific experience available. In this paper, we study pre-task memory construction: whether an agent can build procedural memory before observing any target-environment tasks, using only self-generated synthetic practice. Yet, synthetic interaction alone is insufficient, as without controlling what to practice and what to store, synthetic tasks become redundant, infeasible, and ultimately uninformative, and memory further degrades quickly due to unfiltered trajectories. To overcome this, we present Preping, a proposer-guided memory construction framework. At its core is proposer memory, a structured control state that shapes future practice. A Proposer generates synthetic tasks conditioned on this state, a Solver executes them, and a Validator determines which trajectories are eligible for memory insertion while also providing feedback to guide future proposals. Experiments on AppWorld, BFCL v3, and MCP-Universe show that Preping substantially improves over a no-memory baseline and achieves performance competitive with strong playbook-based methods built from offline or online experience, with deployment cost $2.99\times$ lower on AppWorld and $2.23\times$ lower on BFCL v3 than online memory construction. Further analyses reveal that the main benefit does not come from synthetic volume alone, but from proposer-side control over feasibility, redundancy, and coverage, combined with selective memory updates.

</details>

*Yifei Zhu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）在智能体框架中已将信息检索转变为开放式探索，但现实应用要求模型从分散来源中发现并整合“长尾”事实，这一能力的评估仍然不足。现有研究缺乏对智能体细粒度事实发现与整合能力的系统性基准测试。

Method: 1. 构建PolitNuggets多语言基准数据集，包含400位全球精英的政治传记，覆盖超过10000条政治事实；2. 提出FactNet证据条件协议，建立标准化的多智能体评估系统，从事实发现、细粒度准确性和效率三个维度进行评分；3. 通过基准诊断分析智能体性能与底层模型能力之间的关联。

Result: 实验结果表明：当前系统在处理细粒度细节时经常遇到困难，且不同系统在效率方面差异显著；短上下文提取能力、多语言鲁棒性和可靠工具使用是影响智能体性能的关键因素。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前智能体系统在长尾事实发现与整合方面的局限性，强调了提升短上下文提取、多语言处理和工具使用可靠性的重要性，为未来智能体信息检索系统的优化指明了方向。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) embedded in agentic frameworks have transformed information retrieval from static, long context question answering into open-ended exploration. Yet real world use requires models to discover and synthesize "long-tail" facts from dispersed sources, a capability that remains under-evaluated. We introduce PolitNuggets, a multilingual benchmark for agentic information synthesis via constructing political biographies for 400 global elites, covering over 10000 political facts. We standardize evaluation with an optimized multi agent system and propose FactNet, an evidence conditional protocol that scores discovery, fine-grained accuracy, and efficiency. Across models and settings, we find that current systems often struggle with fine-grained details, and vary substantially in efficiency. Finally, using benchmark diagnostics, we relate agent performance to underlying model capabilities, highlighting the importance of short-context extraction, multilingual robustness, and reliable tool use.

</details>


### [22] [Conditional Attribute Estimation with Autoregressive Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14004)
*Erica Stutz,Giacomo Marino,Daniella Meeker,Qiao Liu,Andrew J. Loza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出条件属性Transformer（CAT），通过单次前向传播同时预测下一token概率和序列级属性值，解决了传统下一token预测目标在全局结构建模上的不足。该方法实现了token级信用分配、反事实分析和可控生成三大能力，在稀疏奖励任务上达到SOTA性能，并显著提升了属性估计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的生成模型采用下一token预测目标进行训练，但许多下游应用需要估计或控制序列级属性。下一token预测会导致训练时局部模式过拟合、全局结构欠拟合，且在推理时需要大量下游修改或昂贵的采样来引导或预测生成样本的全局属性。

Method: 引入条件属性Transformer（Conditional Attribute Transformers），这是一种新颖的方法，可以在单次前向传播中联合估计下一token概率和基于每个潜在下一token选择的属性值，无需修改输入序列。该框架实现了三个关键能力：通过识别序列中每个token如何与属性值关联来实现整个序列的逐token信用分配；通过量化基于不同下一token选择的属性差异来进行反事实分析；通过结合下一token和属性似然来解码序列实现可引导生成。

Result: 该方法在稀疏奖励任务上达到了最先进的性能，在足够的模型规模下改善了下一token预测，比采样方法快数个数量级地估计属性概率，并能够引导自回归序列模型在一系列语言任务上的解码。

Conclusion: 条件属性Transformer成功地将序列级属性估计和可控生成集成到单一模型中，解决了传统下一token预测方法在全局结构建模方面的局限性，为生成模型的可控性和可解释性提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative models are often trained with a next-token prediction objective, yet many downstream applications require the ability to estimate or control sequence-level properties. Next-token prediction can lead to overfitting of local patterns during training, underfitting of global structure, and requires significant downstream modifications or expensive sampling to guide or predict the global attributes of generated samples at inference time. Here, we introduce Conditional Attribute Transformers, a novel method for jointly estimating the next-token probability and the value of an attribute conditional on each potential next token selection. This framework enables three critical capabilities within a single forward pass, without modification of the input sequence: (1) per-token credit assignment across an entire sequence, by identifying how each token in a sequence is associated with an attribute's value; (2) counterfactual analysis, by quantifying attribute differences conditional on alternative next token choices; (3) steerable generation, by decoding sequences based on a combination of next-token and attribute likelihoods. Our approach achieves state of the art performance on sparse reward tasks, improves next-token prediction at sufficient model sizes, estimates attribute probabilities orders of magnitude faster than sampling, and can guide decoding of autoregressive sequence models on a range of language tasks.

</details>


### [23] [Sheaf-Theoretic Transport and Obstruction for Detecting Scientific Theory Shift in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14033)
*David N. Olivieri,Roque J. Hernández*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于有限层论（sheaf-theoretic）的框架，用于检测AI科学代理在理论迁移过程中何时需要范式转换（理论扩展），而非在现有理论框架内调整，并通过过渡卡基准验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI科学代理在理论迁移中面临关键挑战：需要判断现有理论框架在新情境下是否仍然适用，或者其表达语言已出现局部到全局的阻碍而必须扩展。这不仅仅是数据拟合问题，而是涉及理论范式的可迁移性诊断。

Method: 构建有限层论框架，将上下文组织为局部-全局结构，包含源、重叠、目标和验证图表。通过拟合、限制和拼接测试来评估传输可行性。定义了五种阻碍度量：残差拟合失败、重叠不兼容、约束违背、极限关系失败和表征成本。设计了过渡卡基准来区分理论内部变形与理论语言扩展。

Result: 实验表明，直接阻碍排序有效：预期的变形或扩展通常是阻碍最低的候选方案，且过渡类型在基准测试中被成功区分。星座核方法作为次级表征相似性探针被纳入对比。

Conclusion: 该研究并非重建历史范式转换或解决开放式自主理论发明问题，而是为AI代理隔离出了一个有限的诊断子问题：检测表征传输何时失败，以及理论扩展何时成为连贯的下一步行动，为AI科学发现提供了关键的理论转换检测机制。

Abstract: Scientific theory shift in AI agents requires more than fitting equations to data. An artificial scientific agent must detect whether an existing representational framework remains transportable into a new regime, or whether its language has become locally-to-globally obstructed and must be extended. This paper develops a finite sheaf-theoretic framework for detecting theory-shift candidates through transport and obstruction. Contexts are organized as a local-to-global structure in which source, overlap, target, and validation charts are fitted, restricted, and tested for gluing. Obstruction measures failure of coherence through residual fit, overlap incompatibility, constraint violation, limiting-relation failure, and representational cost. We evaluate the framework on a controlled transition-card benchmark designed to separate deformation within a source language from extension of that language. The main result is direct obstruction ranking: the intended deformation or extension is usually the lowest-obstruction candidate, and transition type is separated in the benchmark. A constellation kernel over the same signatures is included only as a secondary representational-similarity probe. The aim is not to reconstruct historical paradigm shifts or solve open-ended autonomous theory invention, but to isolate a finite diagnostic subproblem for AI agents: detecting when representational transport fails and extension becomes the coherent next move.

</details>


### [24] [From Descriptive to Prescriptive: Uncover the Social Value Alignment of LLM-based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14034)
*Jinxian Qu,Qingqing Gu,Teng Chen,Luo Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于价值对齐的框架，利用GraphRAG技术将原则转化为基于价值的指令，在特定对话上下文中检索合适的指令，以引导LLM智能体的行为符合人类价值观。实验显示该方法在DAILYDILEMMAS基准测试上显著优于现有的提示基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的智能体在实际应用中需要与人类社会价值观保持一致，但现有工作在自我认知、困境决策和自我情感方面仍存在不足，难以实现充分的价值对齐。

Method: 提出了一个基于价值的框架，核心是利用GraphRAG（图检索增强生成）技术将抽象原则转化为具体的基于价值的指令。在特定对话上下文中，系统会检索并应用最合适的价值指令来引导智能体的行为。同时，基于马斯洛需求层次理论和普拉切克情绪轮理论定义了预期行为标准。

Result: 在DAILYDILEMMAS基准数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法相比基于提示的基线方法（包括ECoT、Plan-and-Solve和元认知提示）取得了显著的性能提升，有效提高了智能体表现出预期行为的比例。

Conclusion: 该方法成功提升了LLM智能体与人类价值观的对齐程度，为AI系统中自我情感的涌现提供了基础，具有重要的理论价值和实践意义。

Abstract: Wide applications of LLM-based agents require strong alignment with human social values. However, current works still exhibit deficiencies in self-cognition and dilemma decision, as well as self-emotions. To remedy this, we propose a novel value-based framework that employs GraphRAG to convert principles into value-based instructions and steer the agent to behave as expected by retrieving the suitable instruction upon a specific conversation context. To evaluate the ratio of expected behaviors, we define the expected behaviors from two famous theories, Maslow's Hierarchy of Needs and Plutchik's Wheel of Emotion. By experimenting with our method on the benchmark of DAILYDILEMMAS, our method exhibits significant performance gains compared to prompt-based baselines, including ECoT, Plan-and-Solve, and Metacognitive prompting. Our method provides a basis for the emergence of self-emotion in AI systems.

</details>


### [25] [Enhanced and Efficient Reasoning in Large Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14036)
*Leslie G. Valiant*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种高效的原理性推理方法，通过预处理将数据转换为 Unary Relational Integracode，使大模型能够进行可证明的可靠性推理，同时保持现有软硬件基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型虽然能生成流畅文本，但缺乏原理性基础来保证生成内容的可信度。普遍认为添加原理性推理计算成本过高而不可行，本文旨在打破这一认知。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：第一阶段通过预处理将文本数据重新编码为Unary Relational Integracode，显式表达对象间的关系；第二阶段使用标准或简化的机器学习过程来预测这些关系。该方法可视为实现世界模型，通过Robust Logic系统对学习到的（不确定）信息进行原理性推理链式处理。

Result: 证明了该重编码方法具有意外且幸运的特性：虽然简洁，但能使学习训练数据中描述的世界所持有的核心关系规则子集在某种定义下成为多项式时间可学习的（多项式取决于规则复杂度）。这为单次分类器调用内以及多次调用之间的可靠推理提供了支持。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅在计算上实用可行，能够保留现有大量软硬件基础设施，而且为原理性推理提供了理论基础，支持自然语言、视觉和动作等多种模态，证明了在大模型中实现可信推理是可行的。

Abstract: In current Large Language Models we can trust the production of smoothly flowing prose on the basis of the principles of machine learning. However, there is no comparably principled basis to justify trust in the content of the text produced. It appears to be conventional wisdom that addressing this issue by adding more principled reasoning is not computationally affordable.
  Here we propose a principled method of reasoning that is efficient enough to be practical for large language models. Further, the method allows the retention of much of the currently used software and hardware base. Our method for improving the functioning of large language models consists of a first stage of preprocessing that recodes the data to a Unary Relational Integracode that is more explicit about the relationships among the objects described in the text, followed as a second stage by a standard but possibly streamlined machine learning process that then also learns to predict these relationships.
  The method may be viewed as realizing a world model and applying beyond natural language, to vision and actions, for example, where the multiple properties of an object referred to in an input are brought together explicitly, rather than remaining distributed in the various references to it in the input. We articulate its advantages in terms of Robust Logic, a system for performing principled chaining on learned, and hence uncertain, information. We show that this recoding has the surprising and fortuitous property that, while succinct, it makes the task of learning a core subset of relational rules that hold in the world described in the training data polynomial time learnable in a defined sense, the polynomial depending on the complexity of the rule. This gives support for sound reasoning within each single call of the learned classifier as well as between multiple calls.

</details>


### [26] [Model-Adaptive Tool Necessity Reveals the Knowing-Doing Gap in LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14038)
*Yize Cheng,Chenrui Fan,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Keivan Rezaei,Soheil Feiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM工具使用中存在的"知行差距"：模型虽然能判断何时需要工具，但往往无法将这种认知转化为实际行动，导致高达26.5-54.0%的工具调用不匹配率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将工具必要性视为模型无关的属性，但实际中不同能力模型的工具需求边界不同：强模型能独立解决的问题，弱模型可能仍需工具。需要建立模型自适应的工具必要性定义。

Method: 提出基于模型实际表现的自适应工具必要性定义；将工具使用分解为内部认知和执行两个阶段；通过探测LLM隐藏状态分析认知与行动信号的线性可解码性及其在后期层的正交关系；追踪样本在两阶段过程中的轨迹。

Result: 在算术和事实QA数据集上发现26.5-54.0%和30.8-41.8%的工具调用不匹配率；认知和行动信号均可线性解码，但在驱动下一词元的后期层中，两者的探测方向几乎正交；大部分不匹配集中在认知到行动的转换阶段而非认知本身。

Conclusion: LLM工具使用存在"知行差距"：提升工具使用可靠性不仅需要改进对工具需求的识别能力，更需要改善将识别转化为实际行动的能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly act as autonomous agents that must decide when to answer directly vs. when to invoke external tools. Prior work studying adaptive tool use has largely treated tool necessity as a model-agnostic property, annotated by human or LLM judge, and mostly cover cases where the answer is obvious (e.g., fetching the weather vs. paraphrasing text). However, tool necessity in the wild is more nuanced due to the divergence of capability boundaries across models: a problem solvable by a strong model on its own may still require tools for a weaker one. In this work, we introduce a model-adaptive definition of tool-necessity, grounded in each model's empirical performance. Following this definition, we compare the necessity against observed tool-call behavior across four models on arithmetic and factual QA dataset, and find substantial mismatches of 26.5-54.0% and 30.8-41.8%, respectively. To diagnose the failure, we decompose tool use into two stages: an internal cognition stage that reflects whether a model believes a tool is necessary, and an execution stage that determines whether the model actually makes a tool-call action. By probing the LLM hidden states, we find that both signals are often linearly decodable, yet their probe directions become nearly orthogonal in the late-layer, last-token regime that drives the next-token action. By tracing the trajectory of samples in the two-stage process, we further discover that the majority of mismatch is concentrated in the cognition-to-action transition, not in cognition itself. These results reveal a knowing-doing gap in LLM tool-use: improving tool-use reliability requires not only better recognition of when tools are needed, but also better translation of that recognition into action.

</details>


### [27] [Network-Aware Bilinear Tokenization for Brain Functional Connectivity Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14048)
*Leo Milecki,Qingyu Hu,Bahram Jafrasteh,Mert R. Sabuncu,Qingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了NERVE框架，通过将功能连接（FC）矩阵划分为脑网络内部和网络间的连接块进行分词，采用结构化双线性分解方法解决了脑网络模块化组织与自监督学习对齐的问题，在三个大规模队列中表现出更稳定、可迁移的表征学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的掩码自编码器（MAE）方法用于静息态脑功能连接的自监督表征学习时，在分词策略上存在局限性：现有方法通常采用以区域为中心或基于图的方案，将功能连接视为结构同质的元素，忽略了大尺度脑网络的模块化组织结构，未能与大脑网络的内在组织对齐。

Method: 提出NERVE（Network-Aware Representations of Brain Functional Connectivity via Bilinear Tokenization）自监督学习框架。核心创新包括：1）将FC矩阵按照脑网络对划分为网络内部和网络间的连接块作为分词单元，而非传统的固定大小图像块；2）设计结构化双线性分解方法对异构大小的FC块进行嵌入，该方法能够保持网络身份特征，并将参数复杂度从网络数量的二次方降低到线性规模；3）融合解剖学先验的脑分区作为结构化约束。

Result: 在三个大规模发育队列（ABCD、PNC和CCNP）上评估行为和精神病理学预测任务。与结构无关的MAE变体和基于图的自监督基线方法相比，NERVE展现出更稳定、更可迁移的表征，尤其在跨队列评估中表现突出。消融实验证实双线性网络嵌入和解剖学脑分区对性能提升至关重要。

Conclusion: 研究发现表明，在功能连接组学的自监督学习中融入领域特定的结构先验知识（如脑网络的模块化组织）至关重要。NERVE框架成功地将大脑网络的模块化结构整合到MAE框架中，为神经影像领域提供了一种有效结合领域知识与深度学习的方法论范式。

Abstract: Masked autoencoders (MAEs) have recently shown promise for self-supervised representation learning of resting-state brain functional connectivity (FC). However, a fundamental question remains unresolved: how should FC matrices be tokenized to align with the intrinsic modular organization of large-scale brain networks? Existing approaches typically adopt region-centric or graph-based schemes that treat FC as structurally homogeneous elements and overlook the large-scale network brain organization. We introduce NERVE (Network-Aware Representations of Brain Functional Connectivity via Bilinear Tokenization), a self-supervised learning framework that redefines FC tokenization by partitioning FC matrices into patches of intra- and inter-network connectivity blocks. Unlike image-based MAE, where fixed-size patches share a common tokenizer, FC patches defined by network pairs are heterogeneous in size and correspond to distinct functional roles. To resolve this problem, NERVE embeds FC patches through a novel structured bilinear factorization. This formulation preserves network identity and reduces parameter complexity from quadratic to linear scaling in the number of networks. We evaluate NERVE across three large-scale developmental cohorts (ABCD, PNC, and CCNP) for behavior and psychopathology prediction. Compared to structurally agnostic MAE variants and graph-based self-supervised baselines, the proposed network-aware formulation yields more stable and transferable representations, particularly in cross-cohort evaluation. Ablation studies confirm that the proposed bilinear network embedding and anatomically grounded parcellation are critical for performance. These findings highlight the importance of incorporating domain-specific structural priors into self-supervised learning for functional connectomics.

</details>


### [28] [Bridging Legal Interpretation and Formal Logic: Faithfulness, Assumption, and the Future of AI Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14049)
*Olivia Peiyu Wang,Leilani H. Gilpin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号方法，将大语言模型的表达能力与形式化验证的严谨性相结合，以解决LLM在法律推理中过度推断和幻觉问题，实现既强大又可信的法律AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在法律实践中的广泛应用既带来机遇也伴随风险。尽管LLM能够辅助合同推理、文档起草和大规模源分析，但其存在的幻觉问题更为深层：系统性地得出超出源文本支持的推断，将充满假设的结论伪装成逻辑严密的结论。法律工作的高风险性要求AI系统具备当前LLM所缺乏的严谨性。

Method: 采用神经符号方法，将大语言模型的强大表达能力与形式化验证的严谨逻辑相结合，构建混合AI系统。该方法旨在保持AI辅助法律推理能力的同时，引入形式化约束来确保推理过程的可靠性和可追溯性。

Result: 该方案旨在开发出既具备强大法律推理能力又可信赖的AI系统，能够减少人工验证的负担，同时不牺牲法律实践所要求的问责制和严谨性标准。

Conclusion: 通过神经符号方法的融合，可以构建既强大又值得信赖的法律AI辅助系统，在保证法律工作严谨性和问责制的前提下，有效降低人工验证的工作量，为法律实践的AI应用提供可靠的技术路径。

Abstract: The growing adoption of large language models in legal practice brings both significant promise and serious risk. Legal professionals stand to benefit from AI that can reason over contracts, draft documents, and analyze sources at scale, yet the high-stakes nature of legal work demands a level of rigor that current AI systems do not provide. The central problem is not simply that LLMs hallucinate facts and references; it is that they systematically draw inferences that go beyond what the source text actually supports, presenting assumption-laden conclusions as if they were logically grounded. This proposal presents a neuro-symbolic approach to legal AI that combines the expressive power of large language models with the rigor of formal verification, aiming to make AI-assisted legal reasoning both capable and trustworthy, thus reducing the burden of manual verification without sacrificing the accountability that legal practice demands.

</details>


### [29] [SPIN: Structural LLM Planning via Iterative Navigation for Industrial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14051)
*Yusuke Ozaki,Dhaval Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SPIN，一个LLM规划封装器，通过验证DAG规划和前缀执行控制，在保证计划有效性的同时减少不必要的任务执行和工具调用，显著提升了工业LLM智能体系统的效率和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业LLM智能体系统将规划与执行分离，但LLM规划器经常生成结构无效或不必要冗长的工作流，导致脆弱的执行失败和可避免的工具/API成本浪费。

Method: 提出SPIN规划封装器，结合验证式有向无环图(DAG)规划与前缀执行控制：1) 通过_validate_plan_text和修复提示强制执行严格的DAG约束，确保下游执行前生成可执行计划；2) 增量评估DAG前缀，当前缀足以回答查询时停止执行。

Result: 在AssetOpsBench的261个场景中，SPIN将执行任务数从1061降至623，Accomplished指标从0.638提升至0.706，每次运行的工具调用从11.81降至6.82；在MCP Bench上，对GPT OSS1和Llama 4 Maverick均提升了规划、接地和依赖相关得分。

Conclusion: SPIN通过在执行前验证计划结构有效性并在满足查询需求时提前终止执行，成功解决了LLM规划器的结构无效性和冗长问题，实现了更高的执行效率和成功率，为工业LLM智能体系统提供了有效的规划优化方案。

Abstract: Industrial LLM agent systems often separate planning from execution, yet LLM planners frequently produce structurally invalid or unnecessarily long workflows, leading to brittle failures and avoidable tool and API cost. We propose \texttt{SPIN}, a planning wrapper that combines validated Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) planning with prefix based execution control. \texttt{SPIN} enforces a strict DAG contract through \texttt{\_validate\_plan\_text} and repair prompting, producing executable plans before downstream execution, and then evaluates DAG prefixes incrementally to stop when the current prefix is sufficient to answer the query. On AssetOpsBench, across 261 scenarios, \texttt{SPIN} reduces executed tasks from 1061 to 623 and improves \emph{Accomplished} from 0.638 to 0.706, while reducing tool calls from 11.81 to 6.82 per run. On MCP Bench, the same wrapper improves planning, grounding, and dependency related scores for both GPT OSS1 and Llama 4 Maverick.

</details>


### [30] [Bad Seeing or Bad Thinking? Rewarding Perception for Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14054)
*Haozhe Wang,Qixin Xu,Changpeng Wang,Taofeng Xue,Chong Peng,Wenhu Chen,Fangzhen Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MoCA机制，通过解耦感知与推理步骤并引入感知验证方法，解决了视觉语言模型中感知与推理之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLMs在提升感知-推理协同能力时面临"跷跷板效应"：增强推理往往损害感知能力，反之亦然。作者认为根本原因在于模态信用分配的模糊性——当模型失败时，无法确定是感知错误（看错）还是推理错误（想错）。

Method: 1) 将生成过程分解为交替的感知和推理步骤，实现对感知的目标性监督；2) 提出感知验证(PV)，利用"蒙眼推理"代理独立于推理结果奖励感知保真度；3) 设计结构化语言验证(SVV)，用结构化算法执行替代高方差LLM评判；4) 整合为模态感知信用分配(MoCA)机制，将奖励路由至具体错误源。

Result: MoCA机制能够准确识别错误来源（感知或推理），使单一VLM在广泛任务范围内同时获得感知和推理性能的提升，有效消除了传统方法中的"跷跷板效应"。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦感知与推理并实施独立的感知验证，可以突破VLMs中感知-推理权衡的根本瓶颈，实现两者的协同提升。

Abstract: Achieving robust perception-reasoning synergy is a central goal for advanced Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Recent advancements have pursued this goal via architectural designs or agentic workflows. However, these approaches are often limited by static textual reasoning or complicated by the significant compute and engineering burden of external agentic complexity. Worse, this heavy investment does not yield proportional gains, often witnessing a "seesaw effect" on perception and reasoning. This motivates a fundamental rethinking of the true bottleneck. In this paper, we argue that the root cause of this trade-off is an ambiguity in modality credit assignment: when a VLM fails, is it due to flawed perception ("bad seeing") or flawed logic ("bad thinking")? To resolve this, we introduce a reinforcement learning framework that improves perception-reasoning synergy by reliably rewarding the perception fidelity. We explicitly decompose the generation process into interleaved perception and reasoning steps. This decoupling enables targeted supervision on perception. Crucially, we introduce Perception Verification (PV), leveraging a "blindfolded reasoning" proxy to reward perceptual fidelity independently of reasoning outcomes. Furthermore, to scale training across free-form VL tasks, we propose Structured Verbal Verification, which replaces high-variance LLM judging with structured algorithmic execution. These techniques are integrated into a Modality-Aware Credit Assignment (MoCA) mechanism, which routes rewards to the specific source of error -- either bad seeing or bad thinking -- enabling a single VLM to achieve simultaneous performance gains across a wide task spectrum.

</details>


### [31] [MathAtlas: A Benchmark for Autoformalization in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14061)
*Nilay Patel,Noah Arias,Davit Babayan,Victoria Cochran,Timothy Libman,Hafsah Mahmood,Liam McCarty,Soli Munoz,Laurel Willey,Jeffrey Flanigan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布了MathAtlas，首个大规模研究生数学自动形式化基准，包含约5.2万个从103本教科书中提取的定理等实体及约17.8万条依赖关系，实验表明现有最强模型表现不佳，在依赖深度大的子集上正确率仅为2.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动形式化基准主要关注奥林匹克或本科数学，而研究生和研究级数学仍未被充分探索，缺乏大规模、真实场景下的评估资源。

Method: 构建MathAtlas基准数据集，包含从103本研究生数学教科书中提取的约5.2万个定理、定义、练习、示例和证明，并构建包含约17.8万条关系的数学依赖图，用于评估依赖感知的自动形式化系统。

Result: 强基线模型在定理陈述上的正确率最高仅9.8%，定义上为16.7%。在依赖深度最大的MA-Hard子集（700个实体）上，最佳模型正确率仅为2.6%。

Conclusion: MathAtlas是一个高质量但极具挑战性的基准，揭示了现有模型在处理深度依赖关系时的性能衰退，为研究生数学自动形式化研究提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Current autoformalization benchmarks are largely focused on olympiad or undergraduate mathematics, while graduate and research-level mathematics remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce MathAtlas, the first large-scale autoformalization benchmark of in the wild graduate-level mathematics, containing ~52k theorems, definitions, exercises, examples, and proofs extracted from 103 graduate mathematics textbooks. MathAtlas is enriched with a mathematical dependency graph containing ~178k relations, and is the first autoformalization benchmark to include such relations, facilitating evaluation and development of dependency-aware autoformalization systems. Our extensive experiments show that MathAtlas is high quality but extremely challenging: strong baselines achieve at most 9.8% correctness on theorem statements and 16.7% on definitions. Furthermore, we find performance of state-of-the-art models degrades substantially with dependency depth: on MA-Hard, a subset of 700 entities with the deepest dependency trees, the best model achieves only 2.6% correctness for autoformalization on this challenging dataset. We release MathAtlas to the community as a benchmark set for large-scale autoformalization of graduate-level mathematics in the wild.

</details>


### [32] [Know When To Fold 'Em: Token-Efficient LLM Synthetic Data Generation via Multi-Stage In-Flight Rejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14062)
*Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury,Syed Zawad,Feng Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MSIFR框架，在生成过程中间阶段提前检测并终止低质量样本，减少LLM合成数据生成中因后期过滤导致的token浪费，节省高达77%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM合成数据生成流程通常在完整生成输出后才进行质量过滤，导致大量token浪费在最终被丢弃的低质量样本上。

Method: 提出Multi-Stage In-Flight Rejection (MSIFR)框架，将生成过程分解为连续阶段，在中间检查点应用基于规则的快速验证器检测算术不一致、幻觉模式和格式违规等问题，实现早期拒绝低质量样本。该方法无需训练，将in-flight rejection形式化为序贯决策过程，并证明条件效用估计构成鞅，确保早期拒绝不会偏向保留样本的期望效用。

Result: 在5个指令微调模型和7个推理基准上，MSIFR作为独立方法减少11%-77%的token消耗，与早退方法结合可减少高达78.2%，同时保持或提升评估准确率。

Conclusion: MSIFR提供了一种实用的机制，无需额外训练或架构变更，即可显著提高LLM合成数据生成的效率，证明了在生成过程中进行早期质量检测的有效性。

Abstract: While synthetic data generation with large language models (LLMs) is widely used in post-training pipelines, existing approaches typically generate full outputs before applying quality filters, leading to substantial token waste on samples that are ultimately discarded. To address this, we propose Multi-Stage In-Flight Rejection (MSIFR), a lightweight, training-free framework that detects and terminates low-quality generation trajectories at intermediate checkpoints before they reach full completion. MSIFR decomposes the generation process into sequential stages and applies fast rule-based validators to identify arithmetic inconsistencies, hallucination patterns, and formatting violations, enabling early rejection of faulty samples. We formalize in-flight rejection as a sequential decision process and show that any non-trivial discard policy reduces expected token consumption, with stage-wise savings increasing when rejection occurs earlier in the generation pipeline. We further demonstrate that conditional utility estimates form a martingale, ensuring that early, in-flight rejection does not bias the expected utility of retained samples. Across five instruction-tuned models and seven reasoning benchmarks, MSIFR reduces token consumption by 11%-77% as a standalone method, and up to 78.2% when combined with early-exit methods, while preserving or improving evaluation accuracy. These results confirm that MSIFR provides a practical mechanism for improving the efficiency of LLM-based synthetic data generation without additional training or architectural changes.

</details>


### [33] [SkillFlow: Flow-Driven Recursive Skill Evolution for Agentic Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14089)
*Mingda Zhang,Tiesunlong Shen,Haoran Luo,Wenjin Liu,Zikai Xiao,Erik Cambria,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SkillFlow框架，通过基于流的方法解决LLM智能体任务编排中的策略崩塌、信用分配不透明和技能演化盲目等问题，在多个任务上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLA的智能体编排方法面临三大挑战：奖励最大化下的策略崩塌、梯度方差大且信用分配不透明、技能演化缺乏引导（决策通常依赖LLM直接判断而非训练信号）。这些缺陷阻碍了智能体系统的性能和能力增长。

Method: 提出SkillFlow框架，采用可训练的Supervisor作为智能体，构建包含动态技能库和冻结执行器的结构化环境。引入Tempered Trajectory Balance (TTB)损失函数，通过基于回归的流匹配方法按奖励比例采样轨迹，保持编排策略多样性。该方法联合学习反向策略，以零额外推理成本实现透明的逐步信用分配。基于流诊断，设计递归技能演化机制，自主决定演化时机、技能创建/修剪和决策缺口填补。

Result: 在14个数据集上的实验表明，SkillFlow在问答、数学推理、代码生成和真实世界交互决策任务中显著优于所有基线方法，验证了该方法的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: SkillFlow成功实现了从训练信号到自主能力增长的闭环，为LLM智能体系统提供了一种原理性的任务编排框架，有效解决了现有方法的关键瓶颈问题。

Abstract: In recent years, a variety of powerful LLM-based agentic systems have been applied to automate complex tasks through task orchestration. However, existing orchestration methods still face key challenges, including strategy collapse under reward maximization, high gradient variance with opaque credit assignment, and unguided skill evolution whose decisions are typically made by directly prompting an LLM to judge rather than derived from principled training signals. To address these challenges, we propose SkillFlow, a flow-based framework that takes a trainable Supervisor as the agent and a structured environment with dynamic skill library and frozen executor, automating task orchestration through multi-turn interaction. SkillFlow employs Tempered Trajectory Balance (TTB), a regression-based flow-matching loss that samples trajectories proportional to reward, preserving diverse orchestration strategies rather than collapsing to a single mode. The same flow objective yields a jointly learned backward policy that provides transparent per-step credit assignment at zero additional inference cost. Building on these flow diagnostics, a recursive skill evolution mechanism determines when to evolve, what skills to create or prune, and where decision gaps lie -- closing the loop from training signal to autonomous capability growth. Experimental results on 14 datasets show that SkillFlow significantly outperforms baselines across question answering, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and real-world interactive decision making tasks. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SkillFlow-E850.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主语言模型代理集成了规划、工具使用、代码执行等多种能力，虽然实用性增强，但也引入了仅靠最终准确率无法体现的运行故障模式，因此需要深入研究这些系统的可靠性与评估方法。

Method: 提出了ChromaFlow工具增强自主推理框架，该框架围绕规划器导向执行、专业化工具使用和遥测驱动评估构建。研究在GAIA 2023 Level-1验证任务上进行了受控评估，对比了冻结基线配置与扩展编排恢复配置的性能表现。

Result: 基线配置在53个任务中获得29个正确答案（54.72%）。扩展编排配置获得27个正确答案（50.94%），准确率下降，同时增加了回溯、超时、工具失败、调用次数和成本估算。两次随机20任务测试结果为12/20和11/20，显示小规模诊断增益在样本间不稳定。结论为更激进的编排并未改善性能且增加了运行噪音。

Conclusion: 更激进的编排策略并未提升整体性能，反而增加了运行噪音。报告主张应将有界的规划器升级、确定性提取、证据协调和显式运行门控视为可靠自主代理评估的一阶需求。

Abstract: Autonomous language-model agents increasingly combine planning, tool use, document processing, browsing, code execution, and verification loops. These capabilities make agent systems more useful, but they also introduce operational failure modes that are not visible from final accuracy alone. This report presents ChromaFlow, a tool-augmented autonomous reasoning framework built around planner-directed execution, specialized tool use, and telemetry-driven evaluation. We analyze ChromaFlow on GAIA 2023 Level-1 validation tasks under clean evaluation constraints. A frozen full Level-1 baseline achieved 29/53 correct answers, or 54.72%. A later recovery configuration with expanded orchestration achieved 27/53 correct answers, or 50.94%, while increasing tracebacks, timeout events, tool-failure mentions, token-line calls, and campaign-log cost estimates. Two randomized 20-task smoke evaluations produced 12/20 and 11/20 correct answers, showing that small diagnostic gains can be unstable across samples. The central result is therefore a negative ablation: more aggressive orchestration did not improve full-set performance and increased operational noise. The report argues that bounded planner escalation, deterministic extraction, evidence reconciliation, and explicit run gates should be treated as first-order requirements for reliable autonomous agent evaluation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院药师在不确定性、时间压力和患者风险下需做出缓解药物短缺的高风险决策。访谈显示，药师会将注意力集中在小部分药物上，将认知努力仅限于最紧急的情况。这些发现促使研究者正式建立一种有限理性的、注意力引导的决策框架。

Method: 研究者构建了一个注意力引导的决策框架，动态将药物分解为需高成本推理的子集和需低成本监控的互补子集。研究开发了两种智能体：一种是基于药师访谈所得注意力权重的专家智能体（Expert Agent），另一种是通过经验随时间适应注意力分配的学习者智能体（Learner Agent）。通过模拟短至长时间跨度的情景进行验证。

Result: 模拟实验表明，注意力引导的规划能够在无需对完整状态进行推理的情况下支持稳定的决策制定。研究结果显示，主要的决策问题不在于采取何种行动，而在于将认知努力分配至何处。

Conclusion: 注意力引导的满意策略（satisficing strategies）能够降低问题复杂度，同时保持稳定的决策性能，为高压力环境下的药物短缺管理决策提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Hospital pharmacists make high-stakes decisions to mitigate drug shortages under uncertainty, time pressure, and patient risk. Interviews revealed that pharmacists focus attention on a small subset of drugs, limiting cognitive effort to the most urgent cases. Motivated by these findings, we formalize a bounded-rational, attention-guided decision framework that dynamically decomposes drugs into a subset for high-cost reasoning and a complementary subset for low-cost monitoring. We develop two agents: an Expert Agent that applies attention weights derived from pharmacist interviews, and a Learner Agent that adapts attention allocation over time through experience. Across simulated scenarios spanning short to long horizons, we show that attention-guided planning supports stable decision-making without complete state reasoning. These results suggest that a primary decision is not what action to take, but where to allocate cognitive effort, and that attention-guided, satisficing strategies can reduce problem complexity while maintaining stable performance.

</details>

*Yuxiang Lai,Peng Xia,Haonian Ji,Kaiwen Xiong,Kaide Zeng,Jiaqi Liu,Fang Wu,Jike Zhong,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ClawForge，一个用于生成和评估命令行工作流中状态冲突场景的基准框架。在17个场景的测试中，最佳模型严格准确率仅为45.3%，主要瓶颈在于智能体是否在行动前检查现有状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交互式智能体基准测试面临两难困境：人工编写的任务成本高昂且难以扩展，而静态提示评估无法捕捉智能体在持久状态上操作时才会出现的失败。现有基准大多从干净状态初始化任务，缺乏系统性测试智能体如何处理预先存在的部分、过时或冲突产物。

Method: 提出了ClawForge框架，该框架将场景模板、基础槽位、初始化状态、参考轨迹和验证器编译为可复现的任务规范。通过规范化最终状态和可观察的副作用（而非精确轨迹匹配），在持久工作流界面上逐步评估智能体，专门测试状态冲突处理能力。实例化为ClawForge-Bench基准，包含17个场景和6个能力类别。

Result: 对七个前沿模型的测试显示：最佳模型严格准确率仅为45.3%；所有模型的错误状态替换率均低于17%；模型间最大差距（17%到90%）取决于智能体是否在行动前检查现有状态。部分分数和步骤效率分析表明，许多失败是近失闭合而非早期崩溃，且模型在状态冲突下表现出定性不同的失败风格。

Conclusion: 当前前沿模型在处理状态冲突的命令行工作流方面表现不佳，核心问题在于缺乏对现有状态的检查意识。研究表明需要更关注智能体在复杂、有历史遗留状态环境中的决策能力，而不仅仅是清洁环境下的表现。

Abstract: Interactive agent benchmarks face a tension between scalable construction and realistic workflow evaluation. Hand-authored tasks are expensive to extend and revise, while static prompt evaluation misses failures that only appear when agents operate over persistent state. Existing interactive benchmarks have advanced agent evaluation significantly, but most initialize tasks from clean state and do not systematically test how agents handle pre-existing partial, stale, or conflicting artifacts. We present \textbf{ClawForge}, a generator-backed benchmark framework for executable command-line workflows under state conflict. The framework compiles scenario templates, grounded slots, initialized state, reference trajectories, and validators into reproducible task specifications, and evaluates agents step by step over persistent workflow surfaces using normalized end state and observable side effects rather than exact trajectory matching. We instantiate this framework as the ClawForge-Bench (17 scenarios, 6 ability categories). Results across seven frontier models show that the best model reaches only 45.3% strict accuracy, wrong-state replacement remains below 17\% for all models, and the widest model separation (17% to 90%) is driven by whether agents inspect existing state before acting. Partial-credit and step-efficiency analyses further reveal that many failures are near-miss closures rather than early breakdowns, and that models exhibit qualitatively different failure styles under state conflict.

</details>
