Conclusion: 算法公平性评估必须采用多轴框架，仅关注排序性能会忽略导致实际危害的关键失效模式。训练干预与后处理机制相互依赖，需要联合设计和评估以实现真正的公平性。

Abstract: Fairness in toxicity classification involves three integrated axes: ranking, calibration, and abstention. Training-time interventions and post-hoc safety mechanisms cannot be evaluated independently because the former determines the efficacy of the latter. We compare Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM), instance-level reweighting, and Group DRO across these axes, combined with temperature scaling, confidence-based abstention, and per-identity threshold optimization. Evaluation uses subgroup AUC, BPSN/BNSP AUC, error gaps, and per-subgroup Expected Calibration Error (ECE) with bootstrap CIs ($n = 1000$).
  We report four findings. (1) Calibration disparity is a hidden fairness violation. ERM has near-perfect aggregate calibration ($0.013$) but is significantly miscalibrated across all identity subgroups ($+0.029$ to $+0.134$). (2) Training interventions reshape rather than eliminate disparity. Reweighted ERM improves ranking (BPSN AUC $+0.06$ to $+0.12$) but worsens the calibration-fairness gap by up to $+0.232$. Group DRO eliminates calibration disparity but only by becoming uniformly miscalibrated globally (ECE $0.118$). (3) Post-hoc methods inherit training failure modes. Temperature scaling fails because miscalibration is non-uniform. Confidence-based abstention works under ERM but breaks under DRO, where the risk-coverage curve rises with deferral. (4) Abstention itself is unfair. Confidence-based deferral helps background content far more than identity-mentioning content. We argue that SRAI fairness requires a multi-axis framework: methods that differ only in aggregate ranking can differ sharply in failure modes that determine real-world harm.

</details>


### [142] [Rethinking Layer Relevance in Large Language Models Beyond Cosine Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14075)
*Cristian Hinostroza,Rodrigo Toro Icarte,Christ Devia,Andres Carvallo De Ferari,Eugenio Herrera-Berg,Denis Parra,Jorge F Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出余弦相似度是衡量Transformer层重要性的不良指标，建议使用移除层后的实际准确率下降作为更可靠的评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管余弦相似度已被广泛用于评估大语言模型（LLM）中各层的相关性，但作者质疑其作为层重要性代理指标的有效性，旨在寻找更准确的方法来理解模型内部机制并指导模型剪枝。

Method: 首先通过理论分析证明余弦相似度得分低的层仍可能对模型性能至关重要；随后在多个LLM上进行实证研究，分析余弦相似度与实际性能下降之间的相关性；最后提出以移除特定层后模型准确率的实际下降幅度作为评估层相关性的新指标。

Result: 理论分析表明层的余弦相似度得分可以任意低但对该层性能仍起关键作用；实证结果显示余弦相似度与实际性能下降之间的相关性通常较弱或仅为中等，常导致对Transformer内部机制的错误解读；使用实际准确率下降作为指标能更精确地反映层的重要性。

Conclusion: 余弦相似度不适合作为评估LLM层重要性的主要指标，研究界应转向使用基于实际性能下降的指标，这对构建可解释的LLM及制定有效的剪枝策略具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have revolutionized natural language processing. Understanding their internal mechanisms is crucial for developing more interpretable and optimized architectures. Mechanistic interpretability has led to the development of various methods for assessing layer relevance, with cosine similarity being a widely used tool in the field. On this work, we demonstrate that cosine similarity is a poor proxy for the actual performance degradation caused by layer removal. Our theoretical analysis shows that a layer can exhibit an arbitrarily low cosine similarity score while still being crucial to the model's performance. On the other hand, empirical evidence from a range of LLMs confirms that the correlation between cosine similarity and actual performance degradation is often weak or moderate, leading to misleading interpretations of a transformer's internal mechanisms. We propose a more robust metric for assessing layer relevance: the actual drop in model accuracy resulting from the removal of a layer. Even though it is a computationally costly metric, this approach offers a more accurate picture of layer importance, allowing for more informed pruning strategies and lightweight models. Our findings have significant implications for the development of interpretable LLMs and highlight the need to move beyond cosine similarity in assessing layer relevance.

</details>


### [143] [Mini-JEPA Foundation Model Fleet Enables Agentic Hydrologic Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14120)
*Mashrekur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Mini-JEPAs：一种由路由代理调度的多个小型传感器专用基础模型，在专业水文问题上优于单一大型通用模型（如Google AlphaEarth）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的行星尺度地空间基础模型（如Google AlphaEarth）虽然通用性强，但在专业水文信号表征上可能存在妥协，且通常不可访问、成本高昂、需要大规模计算资源。为了解决这些问题，需要一种更高效、专业化的解决方案。

Method: 提出Mini-JEPAs架构：训练五个2200万参数的小型传感器专用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）基础模型，分别处理Sentinel-2光学、Sentinel-1 SAR、MODIS热红外、多时相Sentinel-2物候以及地形-土壤堆栈数据。所有模型共享相同的Vision Transformer骨干网络、JEPA配方和64维输出空间。设计路由LLM根据问题内容选择合适的传感器专用模型。

Result: 各Mini-JEPA在变量重建上表现优异（交叉验证R²：高程0.97、温度0.97、降水0.81）；五个流形在几何结构上差异显著（全局参与比8.9-20.2，局部内在维度2.3-9.0）；地形-土壤和物候模型在土壤湿度、干旱度和降水预测上优于单独的AlphaEarth（ΔR²最高0.031）；路由LLM在精选问题集上实现完美命中率；双检索系统在物理匹配问题上显著优于单独AlphaEarth（Cohen's d=1.10，p=0.031）。

Conclusion: 本地训练的Mini-JEPAs能够在有限计算资源下实现水文智能化应用，通过专业化模型群和路由机制有效解决了大型通用模型在专业领域性能不足的问题，为专业地空间应用提供了可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models compress multispectral observations into dense embeddings increasingly used in natural-language environmental reasoning systems. A single planetary-scale model, e.g. Google AlphaEarth, handles broad characterization well but may compromise on specialized hydrologic signals. Such generalist models are also often inaccessible, expensive, and require large-scale compute. We propose Mini-JEPAs: a fleet of small sensor-specialized Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) foundation models consulted by a routing agent for specialized questions. We pretrained five 22M-parameter Mini-JEPAs sharing an identical Vision Transformer backbone, JEPA recipe, and 64-d output space, using Sentinel-2 optical, Sentinel-1 SAR, MODIS thermal, multi-temporal Sentinel-2 phenology, and a topography-soil stack. Each Mini-JEPA reconstructs the variable matched to its sensor, with cross-validated $R^2$ reaching 0.97 for elevation, 0.97 for temperature, and 0.81 for precipitation. The five manifolds differ in geometric structure, with global participation ratios from 8.9 to 20.2 and local intrinsic dimensionalities from 2.3 to 9.0. Joint topography-soil and phenology models add predictive value beyond AlphaEarth alone for soil moisture, aridity, and precipitation ($ΔR^2$ up to 0.031). A router LLM reads per-modality references and selects appropriate sensors with a perfect hit rate over a curated question set. In paired LLM-as-Judge evaluation, dual retrieval over AlphaEarth and the routed fleet outperforms AlphaEarth alone on physics-matched questions (Cohen's $d = 1.10$, $p = 0.031$). Locally-trained Mini-JEPAs can be operationalized for hydrologic intelligence with modest compute.

</details>


### [144] [Reinforcement Learning for Tool-Calling Agents in Fast Healthcare Interoperability Resources (FHIR)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14126)
*Marius S. Knorr,Robert Müller,Jan P. Bremer,Nils Schweingruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的后训练方法，用于提升LLM在FHIR医疗数据上的多步推理能力，在FHIR-AgentBench基准上将答案准确率从50%（o4-mini基线）提升至77%（Qwen3-8B模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHIR是医疗数据互操作性交换的主流标准，其电子健康记录形成资源的有向图。回答临床问题需要智能体跨多种资源类型进行多步推理、过滤和聚合。先前研究表明，即使配备工具增强的LLM智能体也常选择错误资源或违反遍历约束。

Method: 将FHIR上的推理构建为可查询结构化图上的序贯决策问题。实现多轮CodeAct智能体，使用自定义工具链通过强化学习进行后训练，并采用LLM Judge提供基于执行的奖励信号。构建了端到端的后训练流水线（环境构建、工具链构造、模型训练和自定义评估）。

Result: 在FHIR-AgentBench基准上，RL后训练方法将答案正确率从50%（o4-mini）提升至77%（使用更小更便宜的Qwen3-8B模型），同时在基于提示和闭源模型基线对比中表现更优，且能够强制执行数据完整性约束。

Conclusion: 该端到端后训练流水线能够可靠地改进结构化临床图上的多轮推理能力，证明了强化学习后训练在医疗数据智能体任务中的有效性，为小模型在复杂医疗推理任务中的应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Fast Healthcare Interoperability Resources (FHIR) is the dominant standard for interoperable exchange of healthcare data. In FHIR, electronic health records form a directed graph of resources. Answering clinically meaningful questions over FHIR requires agents to perform multi-step reasoning, filtering, and aggregation across multiple resource types. Prior work shows that even tool-augmented LLM agents (retrieval, code execution, multi-turn planning) often select the wrong resources or violate traversal constraints. We study this problem in the context of FHIR-AgentBench, a benchmark for realistic question answering over real-world hospital data, and frame reasoning on FHIR as a sequential decision-making problem over a queryable structured graph. We implement a multi-turn CodeAct agent and post-train it with reinforcement learning using a custom harness and tools. A LLM Judge provides execution-grounded rewards. Compared to prompt-based, closed-model baselines, RL post-training improves performance while enforcing data-integrity constraints. Empirically, our approach improves answer correctness from 50% (o4-mini) to 77% on FHIR-AgentBench using a smaller and cheaper Qwen3-8B model. We present an end-to-end post-training pipeline (environment building, harness construction, model training and custom evaluation) that reliably improves multi-turn reasoning over structured clinical graphs.

</details>


### [145] [bde: A Python Package for Bayesian Deep Ensembles via MILE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14146)
*Vyron Arvanitis,Angelos Aslanidis,Emanuel Sommer,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: bde是一个专注于表格数据的贝叶斯深度集成Python包，基于MILE方法实现快速训练和不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为表格数据提供一个用户友好的贝叶斯深度集成工具，简化贝叶斯深度学习模型的训练、采样和不确定性量化过程。

Method: 基于JAX高效实现了采样推断方法Microcanonical Langevin Ensembles (MILE)，提供与scikit-learn兼容的估计器接口，支持回归和分类任务的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛采样。

Result: 开发出了bde Python包，实现了快速训练、高效的MCMC采样以及回归和分类任务中的不确定性量化功能。

Conclusion: bde为表格数据的贝叶斯深度学习提供了一个易用高效的解决方案，具有良好的可用性和实用性。

Abstract: bde is a user-friendly Python package for Bayesian Deep Ensembles with a particular focus on tabular data. Built on an efficient JAX implementation of the sampling-based inference method Microcanonical Langevin Ensembles (MILE), it provides scikit-learn compatible estimators for fast training, efficient Markov Chain Monte Carlo sampling, and uncertainty quantification in both regression and classification tasks.

</details>


### [146] [A Systematic Evaluation of Imbalance Handling Methods in Biomedical Binary Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14147)
*Jiandong Chen,Lingjie Su,Le Peng,Yash Travadi,Rui Zhang,Ju Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了五种常用类别不平衡处理方法在生物医学二分类任务中的表现，发现随机过采样、重加权和直接F1优化对复杂模型和非结构化数据有效，而随机欠采样和SMOTE普遍降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学领域的分类任务中普遍存在类别不平衡问题，但不同处理方法在不同模型复杂度和数据模态下的效果尚不清楚，缺乏系统性研究指导实践选择。

Method: 在MIMIC-III（表格）、ADE-Corpus-V2（文本）和MURA（图像）三个公开数据集上，评估随机欠采样、随机过采样、SMOTE、重加权和直接F1优化五种方法。使用从逻辑回归、随机森林到深度神经网络（MLP、BiLSTM、BERT、DenseNet、DINOv2）等多种模型架构，对比原始训练基线。

Result: 简单模型（如表格数据上的逻辑回归）使用不平衡处理方法无显著优势。复杂模型和非结构化数据上：（1）随机过采样和重加权持续提升强大模型性能；（2）直接F1优化主要对文本和图像数据有效；（3）随机欠采样和SMOTE持续降低性能，不推荐使用。

Conclusion: 不平衡处理方法的有效性取决于模型复杂度和数据模型。对于高复杂度模型，选择随机过采样、重加权或直接F1优化可获得显著性能提升，为生物医学分类任务的方法选择提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Objective: The primary goal of this study was to systematically examine the impact of commonly used imbalance handling methods (IHMs) on predictive performance in biomedical binary classification, considering the interplay between model complexity and diverse data modalities.
  Material and Methods: We evaluated five representative IHMs: random undersampling (RUS), random oversampling (ROS), SMOTE, re-weighting (RW), and direct F1-score optimization (DMO), against a raw training (RAW) baseline. The evaluation encompassed three public biomedical datasets: MIMIC-III (tabular), ADE-Corpus-V2 (text), and MURA (image), spanning three common biomedical data modalities. To assess varying model complexity, we employed a range of architectures, from classical logistic regression and random forest to deep neural networks, including multilayer perceptron (MLP), BiLSTM, BERT, DenseNet, and DINOv2.
  Results: For simpler models such as logistic regression on tabular data, IHMs yielded no significant advantage over the RAW baseline, aligning with prior findings. However, clear benefits were observed for more complex models and unstructured data: (a) ROS and RW consistently enhanced the performance of powerful models; (b) direct F1-score optimization demonstrated utility primarily for unstructured text and image data; and (c) RUS and SMOTE consistently degraded performance and are therefore not recommended.
  Conclusion: The effectiveness of IHMs depends on both model complexity and data modality. Performance gains are most pronounced when leveraging appropriate IHMs, such as ROS, RW, and DMO, on high-complexity models.

</details>


### [147] [Uncovering Trajectory and Topological Signatures in Multimodal Pediatric Sleep Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14156)
*Scott Ye,Harlin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过融合几何、拓扑和临床特征增强了多模态嵌入，显著提高了儿科睡眠事件（如呼吸暂停、低通气等）的预测性能和模型校准能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成模型在儿科睡眠分析中显示出潜力，但其多模态嵌入的潜在结构仍不明确，如何提取和利用这些嵌入中的诊断信息是一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: 使用多模态掩码自编码器对30秒儿科PSG（多导睡眠图）时段进行嵌入；通过PHATE派生的逐时段坐标、整夜运动描述符、嵌入云的持久同调摘要以及电子健康记录（EHR）增强嵌入；采用简单的线性模型和MLP模型进行测试，以强调可解释性。

Result: 几何、拓扑和临床特征各自提供了互补的提升；更具表达力的晚期融合模型表现更好，去饱和的AUPRC从0.26提升至0.34，EEG唤醒从0.31提升至0.48，低通气从0.09提升至0.22，呼吸暂停从0.05提升至0.14；完整融合模型在所有四个二元任务中均实现了最佳校准。

Conclusion: 潜在的几何/拓扑信息和EHR提供了超越嵌入的互补且可解释的信号，能够在极端不平衡条件下改善模型的校准和鲁棒性。

Abstract: While generative models have shown promise in pediatric sleep analysis, the latent structure of their multimodal embeddings remains poorly understood. This work investigates session-wide diagnostic information contained in the sequences of 30-second pediatric PSG epochs embedded by a multimodal masked autoencoder. We test whether augmenting embeddings with PHATE-derived per-epoch coordinates and whole-night movement descriptors, persistent homology summaries of the embedding cloud, and EHR yields task-relevant signals. Simple linear and MLP models, chosen for interpretability rather than state-of-the-art performance, show that geometric, topological, and clinical features each provide complementary gains. For binary predictions, feature importance is task-dependent, and more expressive late-fusion models generally perform better, with AUPRC improving from 0.26 to 0.34 for desaturation, 0.31 to 0.48 for EEG arousal, 0.09 to 0.22 for hypopnea, and 0.05 to 0.14 for apnea. We also report Brier score and Expected Calibration Error, where the full fusion model yields the best calibration across all four binary tasks. Our study reveals that latent geometry/topology and EHR offer complementary, interpretable signals beyond embeddings, improving calibration and robustness under extreme imbalance.

</details>


### [148] [Finite Sample Bounds for Learning with Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14168)
*Devin Smedira,Abhijith Jayakumar,Sidhant Misra,Marc Vuffray,Andrey Y. Lokhov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次给出了基于得分匹配学习无界支撑指数族分布结构的非渐近样本复杂度分析，证明了样本界限对模型维度呈多项式依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习无界支撑的连续指数族分布是高维统计中的重要研究方向。得分匹配作为一种计算简便的方法被广泛用于此类分布的学习，但其统计特性的理论理解仍然不足，缺乏非渐近的样本复杂度分析。

Method: 针对多项式指数族分布，使用得分匹配方法进行结构学习，并进行非渐近样本复杂度分析。

Result: 推导出了样本界限，该界限对模型维度呈多项式依赖。这是首个此类结果，先前工作仅提供了渐近样本复杂度界限。

Conclusion: 本文填补了得分匹配在指数族分布学习中的理论空白，为该方法的统计性质提供了首个非渐近样本复杂度保证，具有重要的理论和应用价值。

Abstract: Learning of continuous exponential family distributions with unbounded support remains an important area of research for both theory and applications in high-dimensional statistics. In recent years, score matching has become a widely used method for learning exponential families with continuous variables due to its computational ease when compared against maximum likelihood estimation. However, theoretical understanding of the statistical properties of score matching is still lacking. In this work, we provide a non-asymptotic sample complexity analysis for learning the structure of exponential families of polynomials with score matching. The derived sample bounds show a polynomial dependence on the model dimension. These bounds are the first of its kind, as all prior work has shown only asymptotic bounds on the sample complexity.

</details>


### [149] [LLMs Know When They Know, but Do Not Act on It: A Metacognitive Harness for Test-time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14186)
*Qi Cao,Yufan Wang,Peijia Qin,Shuhao Zhang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种元认知框架，将LLM的FOK和JOL信号转化为推理时的显式控制接口，在无需参数更新的情况下显著提升了Claude Sonnet-4.6在多个基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM通常展现出自我监控信号（如解题前的成功概率估计和解题后的正确性判断），但这些信号往往被孤立测量，未用于控制推理过程。本文旨在探究LLM是否具备可转化为有效测试时控制的潜在元认知能力。

Method: 受Nelson-Narens认知心理学理论启发，提出元认知框架，将监控与推理分离。模型首先报告解题前的“知晓感”（FOK）信号，解题后报告“学习判断”（JOL）信号。框架将这些信号转化为显式控制接口，决定何时信任当前解、何时重试以及何时将多个尝试传递给最终聚合器。

Result: 在文本、代码和多模态推理基准测试中，该框架显著提升了Claude Sonnet-4.6基础模型（无需参数更新或特定微调）。在评估基准上，综合准确率从48.3%提升至56.9%，在HLE-Verified、LiveCodeBench v6和R-Bench-V三个主要评估设置上超越了最强排行榜成绩。

Conclusion: 强大的LLM可能已经具备有用的元认知能力，但需要一个显式的控制框架来在推理过程中加以利用。这为LLM的推理时优化提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often expose useful signals of self-monitoring: before solving a problem, they can estimate whether they are likely to succeed, and after solving it, they can judge whether their answer is likely to be correct. However, these signals are typically measured or elicited in isolation, rather than used to control inference. In this work, we ask whether LLMs possess latent metacognitive ability that can be turned into effective test-time control. Inspired by the Nelson--Narens theory from cognitive psychology, we propose a metacognitive harness that separates monitoring from reasoning. For each problem, the model first reports a pre-solve feeling-of-knowing (FOK) signal; after each solve attempt, it reports a post-solve judgment-of-learning (JOL) signal. Rather than treating these signals as passive confidence estimates, the harness turns them into an explicit control interface for reasoning: it decides when to trust the current solution, when to retry with compact metacognitive feedback, and when to pass multiple attempts to a final aggregator. Across text, code, and multimodal reasoning benchmarks, our harness substantially improves a fixed Claude Sonnet-4.6 base model without parameter updates or benchmark-specific fine-tuning. On the evaluated public benchmark snapshots, it raises pooled accuracy from 48.3 to 56.9 and exceeds the strongest listed leaderboard entries on the three primary evaluation settings: HLE-Verified, LiveCodeBench v6, and R-Bench-V. These results suggest that strong LLMs may already possess useful metacognitive ability, but require an explicit control harness to act on it during reasoning.

</details>


### [150] [How to Scale Mixture-of-Experts: From muP to the Maximally Scale-Stable Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14200)
*Leena Chennuru Vankadara,Moritz Haas,Luke Hayward,Sebastian Bordt,Alessandro Breccia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对混合专家(MoE)架构提出了一种最大尺度稳定参数化(MSSP)方法，解决了现有μP方法在MoE模型中无法保证尺度稳定性和学习率迁移的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿大语言模型主要依赖MoE架构，但缺乏关于超参数如何随网络宽度N、专家宽度Ne、专家数量M、稀疏度K和深度L进行缩放的理论指导，无法确保大规模训练时的稳定性和最优性能。

Method: 1. 建立三种缩放情形的理论框架：I) N≈Ne共缩放、II) N≈M≈K共缩放、III) N, Ne, M, K完全成比例缩放；2. 开发新型动态平均场理论(DMFT)描述MoE的极限训练动力学；3. 基于最大尺度稳定性原则推导MSSP参数化方案。

Result: 实验验证MSSP方法能够在所有三种缩放情形下稳健地实现学习率迁移和随尺度单调改进的性能提升；结合现有深度缩放理论，为MoE架构提供了完整的宽度、深度、专家宽度和专家数量缩放方案。

Conclusion: MSSP参数化方法成功解决了MoE架构中超参数缩放的理论缺口，为大规模MoE模型训练提供了可靠的理论指导和实践方案，填补了现有μP理论在MoE架构中的不足。

Abstract: Recent frontier large language models predominantly rely on Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures. Despite empirical progress, there is still no principled understanding of how hyperparameters should scale with network width $N$, expert width $N_e$, number of experts $M$, sparsity $K$, and depth $L$ to ensure both stability and optimal performance at scale. We take a principled step toward resolving this gap by analyzing three different scaling regimes: (I) co-scaling $N\asymp N_e$, (II) co-scaling $N\asymp M\asymp K$, and (III) full proportional scaling of $N, N_e, M$, and $K$. For each regime, we develop a novel Dynamical Mean Field Theory (DMFT) description of the limiting training dynamics of MoEs that provides a formal foundation for our analysis. Within this framework, we derive the unique parameterization for SGD and Adam satisfying all maximal-update ($μ$) desiderata. We then show that the resulting $μ$P prescription does not reliably induce monotonic improvement with scale or robust learning-rate transfer. We trace these pathologies to scale-dependent observables in the aggregation dynamics, which motivates a refined set of desiderata that we term maximal scale stability. Guided by this principle, we derive a Maximally Scale-Stable Parameterization (MSSP) for both SGD and Adam in all three scaling regimes, and characterize the corresponding limiting dynamics - qualitatively distinct from the $μ$P limit - through a separate DMFT analysis. Experiments verify that MSSP robustly recovers learning rate transfer and monotonic improvement with scale across regimes. Combined with existing depth-scaling theory, these results provide a complete scaling prescription for MoE architectures as a function of width, depth, expert width, and number of experts.

</details>


### [151] [Towards Fine-Grained and Verifiable Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14210)
*Yingying Fang,Haijie Xu,Shuang Wu,Mariathasan Anish,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种细粒度概念瓶颈模型框架，通过将每个概念定位到具体的视觉证据中，解决了现有CBMs无法验证概念预测正确性的问题，在保持预测性能的同时显著提升了透明度和可验证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）虽然提供了可解释性，但无法验证预测的概念是否对应正确的视觉证据，这限制了模型的可靠性，可能导致模型学习到虚假相关性而非预期的概念。

Method: 提出细粒度CBM框架，将每个概念定位（ground）到局部视觉证据中，使用户能够直接检查概念在何处以及如何被编码，从而验证模型是否学习了预期概念而非虚假相关性。

Result: 在医学影像基准测试上的实验表明，该方法学习到的概念空间是信息完备的，预测性能与标准CBMs相当，同时大幅提升了透明度；与事后归因方法不同，该框架能够验证概念表示的存在性和正确性。

Conclusion: 该方法增强了CBMs的可信度，建立了概念层面的人机交互机制，为开发更可靠、临床可操作的概念学习系统奠定了基础，成功弥合了可解释性与可验证性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) offer interpretable alternatives to black-box predictors by introducing human-relatable concepts before the final output. However, existing CBMs struggle to verify whether predicted concepts correspond to the correct visual evidence, limiting their reliability. We propose a fine-grained CBM framework that grounds each concept in localized visual evidence, enabling direct inspection of where and how concepts are encoded. This design allows users to interpret predictions and verify that the model learns intended concepts rather than spurious correlations. Experiments on medical imaging benchmarks show that our learned concept space is information-complete and achieves predictive performance comparable to standard CBMs, while substantially improving transparency. Unlike post-hoc attribution methods, our framework validates both the presence and correctness of concept representations, bridging interpretability with verifiability. Our approach enhances the trustworthiness of CBMs and establishes a principled mechanism for human-model interaction at the concept level, paving the way toward more reliable and clinically actionable concept-based learning systems.

</details>


### [152] [PreFT: Prefill-only finetuning for efficient inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14217)
*Andrew Lanpouthakoun,Aryaman Arora,Zhengxuan Wu,Dhruv Pai,Ben Keigwin,Dan Jurafsky,Christopher Potts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PreFT方法，仅在prefill阶段应用适配器以提升多用户服务吞吐量，在保持性能的同时显著提高服务效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法（PEFTs）在服务多用户个性化模型时存在吞吐量瓶颈，主要原因是decode阶段在处理多个适配器时吞吐量远低于prefill阶段，导致整体服务效率下降。

Method: 提出PreFT（Prefill-only Finetuning），仅在prefill阶段应用适配器，之后丢弃适配器，避免在低吞吐的decode阶段使用适配器。开发了LoRA和ReFT两种prefill-only PEFT的高效实现，并在vLLM推理引擎上发布。

Result: 在Llama 3.1 70B上服务512个适配器时，PreFT的吞吐量是传统PEFT的1.9倍。在监督微调任务中，PreFT评估损失略高，但可通过增加rank来补偿且不影响吞吐量；在强化学习任务中，PreFT与标准PEFT性能接近。

Conclusion: PreFT在准确性与吞吐量之间实现了更优的权衡，验证了prefill-only适配方法比现有PEFT更适合个性化服务场景。

Abstract: Large language models can now be personalised efficiently at scale using parameter efficient finetuning methods (PEFTs), but serving user-specific PEFTs harms throughput, even with specialised kernels and memory management techniques. This is because, theoretically and empirically, a mismatch exists between prefill (processing a large number of tokens at once) and decode (generating a single token autoregressively): the latter has far lower throughput when serving multiple adapters. Rather than optimising performance relative to parameter count, for efficient multi-adapter serving, we instead ought to optimise performance relative to serving throughput. We therefore propose PreFT (Prefill-only Finetuning), wherein we only apply the adapter to prefill tokens and discard it afterwards. PreFT significantly increases throughput with minimal effect on performance. We develop and release an efficient implementation of two prefill-only PEFTs, LoRA and ReFT, on the vLLM inference engine. We first show that serving multi-user PreFTs is more efficient than traditional PEFTs ($1.9\times$ the throughput when serving $512$ adapters on Llama 3.1 70B). Then, we compare the performance of prefill-only vs. all-token adapters on a variety of supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning tasks with LMs at varying scales. On SFT, we observe that the evaluation loss of PreFTs is higher than PEFTs, but can be compensated by increasing rank with nearly no reduction in throughput. On RL, we consistently find that PreFTs approach parity with standard PEFTs. Together, this work validates prefill-only adaptation of LLMs as a more favourable accuracy-throughput tradeoff than existing PEFTs for personalised serving.

</details>


### [153] [Diagnosing Training Inference Mismatch in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14220)
*Tianle Zhong,Neiwen Ling,Yifan Pi,Zijun Wei,Tianshu Yu,Geoffrey Fox,Peng Wu,Xiao Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM强化学习系统中rollout生成与策略优化阶段间的训练-推理不匹配（TIM）问题，证明微小的token级数值差异可独立导致训练崩溃，并提出缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM强化学习系统将rollout生成与策略优化分离，两个阶段理应产生完全一致的token概率。然而实现差异可能导致相同模型权重下对相同序列赋予不同值，引发训练-推理不匹配（TIM）。由于TIM与off-policy漂移和常见稳定机制纠缠，难以检测，因此需要对其进行独立研究。

Method: 在零不匹配诊断设置（VeXact）中隔离TIM问题，使其与off-policy漂移等干扰因素解耦，从而独立研究TIM对训练过程的影响机制。

Result: 微小的token级数值差异可独立导致训练崩溃；TIM会改变有效的优化问题；识别出一组可缓解TIM的补救措施。

Conclusion: TIM并非良性的数值噪声，而是一种系统级扰动，在分析LLM强化学习稳定性时应将其视为一阶因子加以重视和处理。

Abstract: Modern LLM RL systems separate rollout generation from policy optimization. These two stages are expected to produce token probabilities that match exactly. However, implementation differences can make them assign different values to the same sequence under the same model weights, inducing Training-Inference Mismatch (TIM). TIM is difficult to inspect because it is entangled with off-policy drift and common stabilization mechanisms. In this work, we isolate TIM in a zero-mismatch diagnostic setting (VeXact), and show that small token-level numerical disagreements can independently cause training collapse. We further show that TIM changes the effective optimization problem, and identify a set of remedies that could mitigate TIM. Our results suggest that TIM is not benign numerical noise, but a systems-level perturbation that should be treated as a first-order factor in analyzing LLM RL stability.

</details>


### [154] [DT-Transformer: A Foundation Model for Disease Trajectory Prediction on a Real-world Health System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14227)
*Yunying Zhu,Andrew R Weckstein,Kueiyu Joshua Lin,Jie Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DT-Transformer，这是一个在大型医疗系统（MGB，涵盖11家医院和门诊网络，涉及170万患者和5710万条电子病历记录）上训练的基础模型。该模型在下一次事件预测任务中表现出色，在896个疾病类别中达到了中位AUC 0.871，证明了健康系统规模训练在现实世界临床预测中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的疾病轨迹预测对早期干预、资源分配和改善长期预后至关重要。然而，现有基于精心筛选的研究队列训练的模型可能无法反映实际部署环境，而基于单医院数据集训练的模型只能捕捉患者轨迹的片段。这凸显了利用大型多医院医疗系统进行训练和验证的重要性，以更好地反映现实世界的临床复杂性。

Method: 开发了DT-Transformer基础模型，该模型在Mass General Brigham (MGB)医疗系统的结构化电子病历数据上进行训练。数据集包含来自11家医院和广泛门诊网络、覆盖170万患者的5710万条EHR条目。模型采用保留数据集和前瞻性验证设置进行评估。

Result: DT-Transformer在保留数据集和前瞻性验证设置中均实现了强区分能力。在下一次事件预测任务中，在896个疾病类别中达到了年龄和性别分层后的中位AUC 0.871，且所有类别的AUC均超过0.5。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持健康系统规模训练作为开发适用于现实世界临床预测的基础模型的可行路径，证明了利用大规模多医院医疗系统数据进行训练可以有效提升模型在真实临床环境中的适用性和预测性能。

Abstract: Accurate disease trajectory prediction is critical for early intervention, resource allocation, and improving long-term outcomes. While electronic health records (EHRs) provide a rich longitudinal view of patient health in clinical environments, models trained on curated research cohorts may not reflect routine deployment settings, and those trained on single-hospital datasets capture only fragments of each patient's trajectory. This highlights the importance of leveraging large, multi-hospital health systems for training and validation to better reflect real-world clinical complexity. In this work, we develop DT-Transformer, a foundation model trained on 57.1M structured EHR entries over 1.7M patients from Mass General Brigham (MGB), spanning 11 hospitals and a broad network of outpatient clinics. DT-Transformer achieves strong discrimination in both held-out and prospective validation settings. Next-event prediction achieves a median age- and sex-stratified AUC of 0.871 across 896 disease categories, with all categories exceeding AUC 0.5. These results support health system-scale training as a path toward foundation models suited to real-world clinical forecasting.

</details>


### [155] [AudioMosaic: Contrastive Masked Audio Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14231)
*Hanxun Huang,Qizhou Wang,Xingjun Ma,Cihang Xie,Christopher Leckie,Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AudioMosaic，一种基于对比学习的音频自监督学习框架。通过结构化的时频掩码构建正样本对，解决了音频增强设计困难和对比学习需要大批次训练的问题，在多个音频基准测试中取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频自监督学习主要由生成式重建目标驱动，对比学习方法探索较少。主要障碍在于难以设计有效的音频增强方法以及对比预训练需要大批次训练导致的内存消耗问题。作者希望探索对比学习在音频表示学习中的潜力，并解决上述挑战。

Method: AudioMosaic采用对比学习框架，核心创新是使用结构化的时频掩码（structured time-frequency masking）对频谱图块进行处理来构建正样本对。这种设计既减少了内存使用，又实现了高效的大批次训练，避免了传统音频增强方法的设计难题。

Result: AudioMosaic在多个标准音频基准测试上达到了最先进的性能，无论在线性探测还是微调设置下都表现优异。学习到的表示在跨数据集、跨域和跨声学条件下展现出强迁移能力。将其集成到音频-语言模型中，也能提升音频-语言任务的性能。

Conclusion: AudioMosaic证明了对比学习在音频自监督学习中的有效性。与生成式方法相比，该方法学习到的表示具有更强的判别性，尤其在话语级别的表示学习上表现突出，为音频理解任务提供了一个高效且通用的预训练编码器方案。

Abstract: Audio self-supervised learning (SSL) aims to learn general-purpose representations from large-scale unlabeled audio data. While recent advances have been driven mainly by generative reconstruction objectives, contrastive approaches remain less explored, partly due to the difficulty of designing effective audio augmentations and the large batch sizes required for contrastive pre-training. We introduce \textbf{AudioMosaic}, a contrastive learning-based audio encoder for general audio understanding. During pre-training, AudioMosaic constructs positive pairs by applying structured time-frequency masking to spectrogram patches, which reduces memory usage and enables efficient large-batch training. Compared with generative approaches, the AudioMosaic encoder learns more discriminative utterance-level representations that demonstrate strong transferability across datasets, domains, and acoustic conditions. Extensive experiments show that AudioMosaic achieves state-of-the-art performance on several standard audio benchmarks under both linear probing and fine-tuning. We further show that integrating the pretrained AudioMosaic encoder into audio-language models improves performance on audio-language tasks. The code is publicly available in our \href{https://github.com/HanxunH/AudioMosaic}{GitHub repository}.

</details>


### [156] [Active Learners as Efficient PRP Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14236)
*Jeremías Figueiredo Paschmann,Juan Kaplan,Francisco Nattero Santiago Mauricio Barron Bucolo,Juan Wisznia,Luciano del Corro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将PRP重排序重构为主动学习的方法，使用抗噪声排序器替代经典排序算法，显著提升了有限调用预算下的排序质量，并设计了随机方向预言机来消除位置偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Pairwise Ranking Prompting (PRP) 方法使用经典排序算法聚合LLM的成对偏好判断，但存在三个关键问题：(1) 判断结果存在噪声；(2) 判断对顺序敏感；(3) 有时出现不可传递性。这些特性与经典排序算法的假设不匹配。此外，当需要在有限调用预算下截断排序结果时，无法保证可靠的Top-K结果。

Method: 将PRP重排序重构为从噪声成对比较中进行主动学习的问题。提出了抗噪声主动排序器作为经典排序算法的直接替代方案。同时引入了随机方向预言机(randomized-direction oracle)，该方法每次成对比较仅需单次LLM调用，通过随机化方向将系统性位置偏差转化为零均值噪声，从而无需双向调用即可获得无偏的聚合排序。

Result: 在有限调用预算(call-constrained regime)的场景下，提出的主动排序器作为直接替代方案，显著提升了NDCG@10/调用的性能。随机方向预言机成功消除了位置偏差，在保持排序质量的同时降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: 通过将PRP重排序重新定义为主动学习问题，可以更好地处理LLM判断中的噪声、顺序敏感性和不可传递性。主动排序方法在有限预算场景下比传统排序方法更有效，随机方向预言机提供了一种低成本消除位置偏差的解决方案。

Abstract: Pairwise Ranking Prompting (PRP) elicits pairwise preference judgments from an LLM, which are then aggregated into a ranking, usually via classical sorting algorithms. However, judgments are noisy, order-sensitive, and sometimes intransitive, so sorting assumptions do not match the setting. Because sorting aims to recover a full permutation, truncating it to meet a call budget does not produce a dependable top-K. We thus reframe PRP reranking as active learning from noisy pairwise comparisons and show that active rankers are drop-in replacements that improve NDCG@10 per call in the call-constrained regime. Our noise-robust framework also introduces a randomized-direction oracle that uses a single LLM call per pair. This approach converts systematic position bias into zero-mean noise, enabling unbiased aggregate ranking without the cost of bidirectional calls.

</details>


### [157] [Paraphrasing Attack Resilience of Various AI-Generated Text Detection Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14240)
*Andrii Shportko,Inessa Verbitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器生成文本检测方法在改写攻击下的鲁棒性，发现包含Binoculars的集成模型检测性能最强但抗攻击能力最弱，揭示了当前AI文本检测领域中高性能与高鲁棒性难以兼得的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的广泛应用，抄袭和虚假信息传播等问题日益严重，加之AI检测规避工具的兴起，迫切需要可靠的机器生成文本检测技术。然而现有检测方法的抗攻击能力尚不明确，因此需要系统评估其在改写攻击下的表现。

Method: 研究评估了三种机器生成文本检测方法的改写攻击韧性：微调RoBERTa、Binoculars和文本特征分析，并使用随机森林分类器构建它们的集成模型。通过对比各方法在攻击前后的性能变化，分析检测效果与抗攻击能力的关系。

Result: 实验发现，包含Binoculars的集成模型具有最强的检测性能，但在遭受改写攻击时性能损失也最为严重。研究揭示了AI文本检测中存在性能与韧性之间的二分法矛盾：高准确率的检测方法往往伴随较低的抗攻击能力。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的AI文本检测技术存在可靠性的认知偏差，高性能与高鲁棒性之间存在权衡关系，这对机器生成文本检测技术的实际应用和可靠性评估提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: The recent large-scale emergence of LLMs has left an open space for dealing with their consequences, such as plagiarism or the spread of false information on the Internet. Coupling this with the rise of AI detector bypassing tools, reliable machine-generated text detection is in increasingly high demand. We investigate the paraphrasing attack resilience of various machine-generated text detection methods, evaluating three approaches: fine-tuned RoBERTa, Binoculars, and text feature analysis, along with their ensembles using Random Forest classifiers. We discovered that Binoculars-inclusive ensembles yield the strongest results, but they also suffer the most significant losses during attacks. In this paper, we present the dichotomy of performance versus resilience in the world of AI text detection, which complicates the current perception of reliability among state-of-the-art techniques.

</details>


### [158] [Latency-Quality Routing for Functionally Equivalent Tools in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14241)
*Kexin Chu,Dawei Xiang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了LQM-ContextRoute，一种用于同功能工具提供商选择的上下文赌博机路由器。通过“延迟-质量匹配”设计，将延迟视为服务容量而非加性奖励，结合查询特定的质量估计与LLM-as-judge反馈，在运行时压力与提供商质量差异共存时显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强的LLM代理越来越多地通过多个功能等效的提供商访问同一类工具（如网络搜索API、检索器或LLM后端）。这带来了运行时负载下的提供商路由问题：路由器需要在延迟、可靠性和答案质量各异的提供商间做选择，且部署时通常缺乏黄金标签。现有的加性奖励机制存在缺陷：低延迟可能掩盖糟糕的答案质量。

Method: 提出LQM-ContextRoute，一种上下文赌博机路由器。其核心设计是延迟-质量匹配（Latency-Quality Matching）：不将低延迟作为加性奖励抵消较差答案，而是根据每个服务周期的预期答案质量对提供商进行排名。该方法将这种容量感知评分与查询特定的质量估计以及LLM-as-judge反馈相结合，使路由器能够在线适应负载变化和提供商质量差异。

Result: 在Web搜索负载基准测试中，L1相比SW-UCB提升+2.18 pp，且保持在延迟-质量前沿；在高异构性StrategyQA设置中，避免了加性奖励崩塌，准确率相比SW-UCB提升高达+18 pp；在异构检索器池上，NDCG相比SW-UCB提升+2.91--+3.22 pp。

Conclusion: 同功能工具路由受益于将延迟视为服务容量（service capacity），特别是在运行时压力与提供商质量异构性共存时，延迟-质量匹配设计能避免加性奖励崩塌问题，实现更好的路由效果。

Abstract: Tool-augmented LLM agents increasingly access the same tool type through multiple functionally equivalent providers, such as web-search APIs, retrievers, or LLM backends exposed behind a shared interface. This creates a provider-routing problem under runtime load: the router must choose among providers that differ in latency, reliability, and answer quality, often without gold labels at deployment time. We introduce LQM-ContextRoute, a contextual bandit router for same-function tool providers. Its key design is latency-quality matching: instead of letting low latency offset poor answers in an additive reward, the router ranks providers by expected answer quality per service cycle. It combines this capacity-aware score with query-specific quality estimation and LLM-as-judge feedback, allowing it to adapt online to both load changes and provider-quality differences. On the main web-search load benchmark, LQM-ContextRoute improves F1 by +2.18 pp over SW-UCB while staying on the latency-quality frontier. In a high-heterogeneity StrategyQA setting, LQM-ContextRoute avoids additive-reward collapse and improves accuracy by up to +18 pp over SW-UCB; on heterogeneous retriever pools, it improves NDCG by +2.91--+3.22 pp over SW-UCB. These results show that same-function tool routing benefits from treating latency as service capacity, especially when runtime pressure and provider-quality heterogeneity coexist.

</details>


### [159] [Artificial Intelligence-Assistant Cardiotocography: Unified Model for Signal Reconstruction, Fetal Heart Rate Analysis, and Variability Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14242)
*Xiaohua Wang,Kai Yu,XuXiao Liang,Liang Wang,Chao Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI的FHrCTG模型，通过大规模数据预训练和专家标注微调，实现了高精度的胎心率信号重建与监测。模型在检测关键胎心率变化方面表现出高敏感性和特异性，并在临床应用指标上取得优异的AUC分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的胎心率监测方法受限于设备性能、数据传输以及医生主观评估等因素，存在局限性。为预防胎儿窘迫和不良结局，需要开发更准确、客观的胎心率监测方法。

Method: 开发了一种专门用于胎心率监测的AI模型FHrCTG，首先在558,412个无标注数据点上进行预训练，然后在7,266个专家审核条目上进行微调。引入了交叉重叠标签(IOL)方法，将心率分析转化为分类判断，有效减轻噪声干扰并精确重建信号。

Result: 模型在检测关键胎心率减速时达到89.13%的敏感性和87.78%的特异性，在检测加速时达到62.5%的敏感性和92.04%的特异性。基于Fischer临床应用标准，模型在验证胎心率周期性和振幅变化方面的AUC分数分别为0.7214和0.9643。

Conclusion: 该AI模型能够有效克服传统方法的局限性，在胎心率监测和变异性评估方面表现出色，具有较高的临床应用价值，有望改善胎儿监护质量并降低不良结局风险。

Abstract: The monitoring of fetal heart rate (FHR) and the assessment of its variability are crucial for preventing fetal compromise and adverse outcomes. However, traditional methods encounter limitations arising from equipment performance, data transmission, and subjective assessments by doctors. We have developed a tailored AI-based FHrCTG model specifically for FHR monitoring, which effectively mitigates noise interference and precisely reconstructs signals. Our model was pre-trained on a massive dataset consisting of 558,412 unlabeled data points and further refined using 7,266 expert-reviewed entries. To validate FHR, we introduced the Intersection Overlapping Labels (IOL) approach, which transforms rate analysis into categorical judgments. Testing revealed that our model demonstrates high sensitivity and specificity in detecting critical FHR decelerations (89.13% and 87.78%, respectively) and accelerations (62.5% and 92.04%, respectively). Furthermore, based on Fischer's criteria for clinical application, our model achieved impressive AUC scores of 0.7214 and 0.9643 for verifying FHR periodicity and amplitude variation, respectively.

</details>


### [160] [Action-Conditioned Risk Gating for Safety-Critical Control under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14246)
*Yushen Liu,Yin-Jen Chen,Ziyi Chen,Tao Wang,Heng Huang,Xugui Zhou,Yanfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的“风险门控”强化学习方法，用于解决部分可观测环境下的风险敏感控制问题，在血糖调节和导航任务中均表现出优于基准方法的性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多安全关键控制问题被建模为风险敏感的部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）。虽然信念空间规划提供了理论上的解决方案，但在实际应用中，维护和规划信念分布计算成本极高，且对模型规格敏感，因此迫切需要一种轻量级且实用的近似方法。

Method: 提出风险门控强化学习方法：首先构建一个紧凑的有限历史代理状态，并训练一个动作条件的近期安全违规预测器；随后将该预测风险以两种互补方式利用：一是在价值学习过程中作为风险惩罚项，二是作为决策时的门控机制，根据风险高低在乐观与保守的集成价值估计之间进行插值，从而实现低风险动作偏向回报寻求，高风险动作偏向保守估计。

Result: 在自动化血糖调节和安全约束导航两个安全关键部分可观测领域进行了评估。在成人和青少年血糖控制队列中，该方法改善了血糖平衡并大幅降低了运行时间（相对于信念空间规划基准）；在Safety-Gym导航基准测试中，相比无约束强化学习和多种标准安全强化学习基准，实现了更优的奖励-成本平衡。

Conclusion: 研究表明，动作条件的近期风险信号可以作为有效的局部信号，用于近似风险敏感的POMDP控制，特别是在完整信念空间规划不可行的实际场景中，提供了一种高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Many safety-critical control problems are modeled as risk-sensitive partially observable Markov decision processes, where the controller must make decisions from incomplete observations while balancing task performance against safety risk. Although belief-space planning provides a principled solution, maintaining and planning over beliefs can be computationally costly and sensitive to model specification in practical domains. We propose a lightweight risk-gated reinforcement learning approximation for risk-sensitive control under partial observability. The method constructs a compact finite-history proxy state and learns an action-conditioned predictor of near-term safety violation. This predicted candidate-action risk is used in two complementary ways: as a risk penalty during value learning, and as a decision-time gate that interpolates between optimistic and conservative ensemble value estimates. As a result, low-risk actions are evaluated closer to reward-seeking estimates, while high-risk actions are evaluated more conservatively. We evaluate the approach in two safety-critical partially observable domains: automated glucose regulation and safety-constrained navigation. Across adult and adolescent glucose-control cohorts, the method improves overall glycemic tradeoffs and substantially reduces runtime relative to a belief-space planning baseline. On Safety-Gym navigation benchmarks, it achieves a more favorable reward-cost balance than unconstrained RL and several standard safe-RL baselines. These results suggest that action-conditioned near-term risk can provide an effective local signal for approximate risk-sensitive POMDP control when full belief-space planning is impractical.

</details>


### [161] [EnergyLens: Predictive Energy-Aware Exploration for Multi-GPU LLM Inference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14249)
*Zhiye Song,Kyungmi Lee,Eun Kyung Lee,Xin Zhang,Tamar Eilam,Anantha P. Chandrakasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EnergyLens框架，用于LLM推理的能耗预测与优化，在多GPU配置下实现9.25%-13.19%的预测误差，并发现配置间能耗差异高达52.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM规模扩大，预测和降低其能耗对可持续性和数据中心运营至关重要。现有方法要么需要生产级代码和昂贵的性能分析，要么无法准确捕捉多GPU能耗行为，导致实践者缺乏工具来决定优化优先级和选择部署配置。

Method: 提出EnergyLens端到端框架，采用基于einsum的接口捕获LLM规范（包括融合、并行和计算-通信重叠），结合负载不平衡感知的MoE建模和经验驱动的多GPU通信能耗模型。

Result: 在Llama3和Qwen3-MoE上验证，张量并行和专家并行配置下多GPU prefill和decode能耗预测的MAPE为9.25%-13.19%，Megatron风格重叠的SM分配预测MAPE为12.97%。发现配置间能耗差异在prefill和decode效率上分别达1.47倍和52.9倍，成功识别Pareto最优重叠配置。

Conclusion: EnergyLens填补了LLM能耗预测工具的空白，能够准确预测多GPU场景下的能耗，揭示计算-通信重叠难以通过直觉优化，但框架能正确识别最优配置，为分布式服务决策提供依据。

Abstract: We present EnergyLens, an end-to-end framework for energy-aware large language model (LLM) inference optimization. As LLMs scale, predicting and reducing their energy footprint has become critical for sustainability and datacenter operations, yet existing approaches either require production-level code and expensive profiling or fail to accurately capture multi-GPU energy behavior. As a result, practitioners lack tools for deciding which optimizations to prioritize and for selecting among existing deployment configurations when exhaustive profiling is impractical. EnergyLens addresses this gap with an intuitive einsum-based interface that captures LLM specifications including fusion, parallelism, and compute-communication overlap, combined with load-imbalance-aware MoE modeling and an empirically driven communication energy model for multi-GPU settings. We validate EnergyLens on Llama3 and Qwen3-MoE across tensor-parallel and expert-parallel configurations, achieving mean absolute percentage errors (MAPEs) between 9.25% and 13.19% for multi-GPU prefill and decode energy, and 12.97% across SM allocations for Megatron-style overlap. Our energy-driven exploration reveals up to 1.47x and 52.9x energy variation across configurations in prefill and decode efficiency and motivates distributed serving. We further show that compute-communication overlap is difficult to optimize with intuition alone, but EnergyLens correctly identifies Pareto-optimal overlap configurations.

</details>


### [162] [Not All Timesteps Matter Equally: Selective Alignment Knowledge Distillation for Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14252)
*Kai Sun,Peibo Duan,Yongsheng Huang,Guowei Zhang,Benjamin Smith,Nanxu Gong,Levin Kuhlmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性对齐知识蒸馏方法(SeAl-KD)，通过针对性地纠正错误时间步的对齐目标，解决了现有SNN蒸馏方法中统一对齐所有时间步导致的问题，在静态图像和神经形态数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN知识蒸馏方法通常对所有时间步进行统一对齐，隐含假设每个时间步的预测应被同等对待。然而SNN的预测随时间演化，即使最终聚合输出正确，中间时间步也未必都需要单独正确。强制所有时间步对齐同一监督目标会破坏有用的时间动态特性，因此需要设计能够选择性纠正错误时间步的蒸馏方法。

Method: 提出选择性对齐知识蒸馏(SeAl-KD)方法：1) 在错误时间步上均衡竞争性逻辑值来选择性对齐类别级知识；2) 基于置信度和时间步间相似性重新加权时间对齐，从而在纠正错误时间步的同时保留有用的时间动态特性。

Result: 在静态图像数据集和神经形态事件数据集上进行了大量实验，结果表明该方法相比现有蒸馏方法取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: SeAl-KD方法通过选择性对齐机制，既能够纠正错误时间步的预测，又能够保留有益的时间动态信息，为缩小SNN与ANN之间的性能差距提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs), which are brain-inspired and spike-driven, achieve high energy efficiency. However, a performance gap between SNNs and artificial neural networks (ANNs) still remains. Knowledge distillation (KD) is commonly adopted to improve SNN performance, but existing methods typically enforce uniform alignment across all timesteps, either from a teacher network or through inter-temporal self-distillation, implicitly assuming that per-timestep predictions should be treated equally. In practice, SNN predictions vary and evolve over time, and intermediate timesteps need not all be individually correct even when the final aggregated output is correct. Under such conditions, effective distillation should not force every timestep toward the same supervision target, but instead provide corrective guidance to erroneous timesteps while preserving useful temporal dynamics. To address this issue, we propose Selective Alignment Knowledge Distillation (SeAl-KD), which selectively aligns class-level and temporal knowledge by equalizing competing logits at erroneous timesteps and reweighting temporal alignment based on confidence and inter-timestep similarity. Extensive experiments on static image and neuromorphic event-based datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over existing distillation methods. The code is available at https://github.com/KaiSUN1/SeAl

</details>


### [163] [Architecture-Aware Explanation Auditing for Industrial Visual Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14255)
*Sibo Jia,Zihang Zhao,Kunrong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于原生读出假设的解释审计框架，发现ViT+Attention Rollout在删除AUC上显著优于其他组合（0.211 vs 0.432-0.525），且RISE这种模型无关方法表现最佳。解释方法的忠实度取决于（模型、解释器、扰动算子）三者的联合属性，而非单一因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业视觉检测系统中的深度学习分类器所生成的热力图解释虽然看起来合理，但往往无法真正识别驱动模型决策的图像区域，存在解释与实际决策机制不一致的问题，需要建立严格的审计协议来评估和改进解释方法的忠实度。

Method: 提出基于原生读出假设的架构感知解释审计协议，在WM-811K晶圆图数据集（9类，172k图像）上采用三种子零填充扰动协议，系统性比较ViT-Tiny+Attention Rollout、Swin-Tiny/ResNet18+CBAM/DenseNet121+Grad-CAM等模型-解释器组合，并引入RISE作为模型无关控制方法，同时进行模糊填充敏感性分析和边界条件探索研究。

Result: ViT-Tiny+Attention Rollout达到删除AUC 0.211，显著优于Swin-Tiny/ResNet18+CBAM/DenseNet121+Grad-CAM的0.432-0.525（Cohen's d绝对值>1.1）；Swin-Tiny作为Transformer却与Grad-CAM兼容，证明关键因素是读出结构而非架构家族；RISE将所有模型家族压缩至删除AUC约0.1，超越所有原生方法；模糊填充扰动下家族排序反转，证明忠实度是三元组的联合属性。

Conclusion: 解释方法的忠实度受原生读出结构约束，是一种兼容性原则而非最优性保证；解释通路应基于读出结构与模型架构协同设计；部署的热力图应附带定量的忠实度指标；审计结果具有数据集/任务依赖性，需要根据具体条件进行限定。

Abstract: Industrial visual inspection systems increasingly rely on deep classifiers whose heatmap explanations may appear visually plausible while failing to identify the image regions that actually drive model decisions. This paper operationalizes an architecture-aware explanation audit protocol grounded in the native-readout hypothesis: the perturbation-based faithfulness of an explanation method is bounded by its structural distance from the model's native decision mechanism. On WM-811K wafer maps (9 classes, 172k images) under a three-seed zero-fill perturbation protocol, ViT-Tiny + Attention Rollout attains Deletion AUC 0.211 against 0.432-0.525 for Swin-Tiny / ResNet18+CBAM / DenseNet121 + Grad-CAM (abs(Cohen's d) > 1.1), despite lower classification accuracy. Swin-Tiny disentangles architecture family from readout structure: despite being a Transformer, its spatial feature-map hierarchy makes it Grad-CAM compatible, showing that the operative factor is readout structure rather than architecture family. A model-agnostic control (RISE) compresses all families to Deletion AUC about 0.1, indicating the gap arises from the explainer pathway; notably, RISE outperforms all native methods, so native readout is a compatibility principle rather than an optimality guarantee. A blur-fill sensitivity analysis shows that the family ordering reverses under a different perturbation baseline, reinforcing that faithfulness rankings are joint properties of (model, explainer, perturbation operator) triples. An exploratory boundary-condition study on MVTec AD (pretrained models) indicates that audit results are dataset/task dependent and identifies conditions requiring qualification. The protocol yields actionable guidance: explanation pathways should be co-designed with model architectures based on readout structure, and deployed heatmaps should be accompanied by quantitative faithfulness metrics.

</details>


### [164] [Dynamics of the Transformer Residual Stream: Coupling Spectral Geometry to Network Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14258)
*Jesseba Fernando,Grigori Guitchounts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对三个生产级大语言模型进行完整的雅可比矩阵特征分解，揭示了训练过程中形成的谱几何结构：从早期的非正规、旋转主导层到晚期的近似对称层，存在单调的谱梯度，以及导致维度坍缩的低秩瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将大语言模型的深度视为离散时间，将残差流视为动力系统，但依赖标量摘要或近似线性化方法，导致对训练后LLM的完整谱几何结构理解不足。本文旨在揭示LLM中完整的谱几何特性及其与网络功能拓扑的联系。

Method: 对三个生产级LLM进行完整的雅可比矩阵特征分解，分析谱结构随深度的演化规律，通过消融实验验证谱特性的来源，并研究图谱社区拓扑与雅可比矩阵行为之间的关联。

Result: 发现训练在LLM中建立了单调的谱梯度——从早期的非正规、旋转主导层过渡到晚期的近似对称层；存在累积的低秩瓶颈，将扰动压缩到残差流的少量有效维度；这种梯度与维度坍缩是学习获得的而非架构固有；图谱社区的拓扑位置能预测雅可比矩阵对其的放大或抑制效应。

Conclusion: 本文绘制了LLM中学习得到的谱几何图谱，将扰动传播、压缩与网络的功能拓扑联系起来，为理解LLM的计算传播机制提供了新的理论视角和实证依据。

Abstract: Large language models are remarkably capable, yet how computation propagates through their layers remains poorly understood. A growing line of work treats depth as discrete time and the residual stream as a dynamical system, where each layer's nonlinear update has a local linear description. However, previous analyses have relied on scalar summaries or approximate linearizations, leaving the full spectral geometry of trained LLMs unknown. We perform full Jacobian eigendecomposition across three production--scale LLMs and show that training installs a monotonic spectral gradient through depth -- from non-normal, rotation-dominated early layers to near--symmetric late layers -- together with a cumulative low-rank bottleneck that funnels perturbations into a small fraction of the residual stream's effective dimensions. Our experiments reveal that this gradient and the dimensional collapse are learned rather than architectural, and is largely dissolved when structured non-normality is removed. We further show that the topological positioning of graph communities predicts whether the Jacobian amplifies or suppresses them, with the sign of the coupling determined by the local operator type, a relationship absent at initialization. These results map a learned spectral geometry in LLMs that links perturbation propagation and compression to the network's functional topology.

</details>


### [165] [TILT: Target-induced loss tilting under covariate shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14280)
*Kakei Yamamoto,Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TILT方法用于协变量偏移下的无监督域适应，通过分解源预测器并惩罚目标域上的辅助分量，实现了优于传统重要性加权方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协变量偏移下的无监督域适应中，传统的域间差异最小化方法常导致目标预测器欠定，而重要性加权方法需要估计密度比，在实践中往往不稳定。因此需要一种既能隐式实现重要性加权，又避免直接密度比估计的方法。

Method: 提出目标诱导损失倾斜（TILT）方法：将源预测器分解为f+b，在标注源数据上拟合f+b，同时在未标注目标输入上惩罚辅助分量b。最终部署f作为目标预测器。该目标域惩罚在总体水平上隐式诱导相对重要性加权，但使用自定位到当前误差的估计量b*_f，对于任意源-目标对（包括支撑集不相交的情况）保持一致有界。

Result: 证明了总体水平上隐式重要性加权的性质；建立了超额风险的一般有限样本oracle不等式；为稀疏ReLU网络训练提供端到端保证。实验上，在受控回归问题和偏移的CIFAR-100蒸馏任务中，TILT相比仅源训练、精确重要性加权和相对密度比基线，在目标域性能上有提升，且对正则化参数依赖稳定。

Conclusion: TILT方法通过新颖的目标函数分解和惩罚机制，成功实现了无监督域适应，在理论和实验上都展现出优于传统方法的优势，为协变量偏移下的域适应问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce and analyze Target-Induced Loss Tilting (TILT) for unsupervised domain adaptation under covariate shift. It is based on a novel objective function that decomposes the source predictor as $f+b$, fits $f+b$ on labeled source data while simultaneously penalizing the auxiliary component $b$ on unlabeled target inputs. The resulting fit $f$ is deployed as the final target predictor. At the population level, we show that this target-side penalty implicitly induces relative importance weighting at the population level, but in terms of an estimand $b^*_f$ that is self-localized to the current error, and remains uniformly bounded for any source-target pair (even those with disjoint supports). We prove a general finite-sample oracle inequality on the excess risk, and use it to give an end-to-end guarantee for training with sparse ReLU networks. Experiments on controlled regression problems and shifted CIFAR-100 distillation show that TILT improves target-domain performance over source-only training, exact importance weighting, and relative density-ratio baselines, with a stable dependence on the regularization parameter.

</details>


### [166] [Smooth Multi-Policy Causal Effect Estimation in Longitudinal Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14284)
*Wenxin Chen,Weishen Pan,Kyra Gan,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多动态治疗策略评估中传统独立估计方法存在的缺陷，提出了一种基于共享表征的联合估计框架PEQNet，有效控制了二阶偏差，显著降低了有限样本方差和误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗和政策决策中，经常需要比较评估多个动态治疗策略。然而，传统的纵向因果推断方法对每个策略进行独立估计，无法在反事实之间共享信息，这会导致结构性的、不可控的二阶偏差，即使在经过标准LTMLE去偏后，仍会放大有限样本方差。

Method: 提出了一种策略感知的迭代条件期望（ICE）Q函数重参数化方法，实现了通过共享表征进行联合估计。构建了以共享策略编码器为核心的策略编码Q网络（PEQNet），利用核均值嵌入训练编码器，使学习到的表征空间反映群体层面的策略差异。最后应用LTMLE校正步骤。

Result: 证明了该设计对二阶余项施加了结构性约束，从而稳定了有限样本方差。在半合成数据集上的实验表明，PEQNet始终优于现有的基于ICE的方法，均方根误差显著降低，特别是在评估相似策略时效果更佳。

Conclusion: 本文提出的PEQNet框架成功解决了多动态治疗策略评估中的信息共享问题，通过联合估计和结构约束有效控制了二阶偏差，为医疗和政策决策提供了更准确、稳定的评估工具。

Abstract: Comparative evaluation of multiple dynamic treatment policies is essential for healthcare and policy decisions, yet conventional longitudinal causal inference methods estimate each in isolation, preventing information sharing across counterfactuals. We demonstrate that this separate estimation paradigm induces a structurally uncontrolled second-order bias, inflating finite-sample variance even after standard debiasing with longitudinal targeted maximum likelihood estimation(LTMLE). To address this, we propose a policy-aware reparameterization of Iterative Conditional Expectation (ICE) Q-functions that enables joint estimation through shared representations. We implement this approach in the Policy-Encoded Q Network (PEQ-Net), an architecture centered on a shared policy encoder. The encoder is trained using kernel mean embeddings, ensuring that the learned representation space reflects population-level policy dissimilarities. After applying an LTMLE correction step, we prove this design imposes a structural constraint on the second-order remainder, thereby stabilizing finite-sample variance. Experiments on semi-synthetic datasets demonstrate that PEQ-Net consistently outperforms existing ICE-based methods, achieving substantial reductions in root-mean-square error, particularly when evaluating closely related policies.

</details>


### [167] [MetaMoE: Diversity-Aware Proxy Selection for Privacy-Preserving Mixture-of-Experts Unification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14289)
*Weisen Jiang,Shuhao Chen,Sinno Jialin Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MetaMoE框架，通过使用公开代理数据作为不可访问私有数据的替代，将独立训练的领域专家统一到单一的MoE模型中，解决了隐私约束下的分布式数据统一训练问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)模型大多假设训练数据集中存储，但在实际应用中，数据通常分布在多个客户端且因隐私约束无法共享，这使得统一的MoE训练面临挑战。

Method: 提出MetaMoE框架，核心是多样性感知的代理选择机制：从公开数据中选择与客户端领域相关且多样的样本作为代理，用于近似私有数据分布并指导路由器学习；同时使用这些代理对齐专家训练，并引入上下文感知路由器以增强异构输入的专家选择能力。

Result: 在计算机视觉和自然语言处理基准测试上的实验表明，MetaMoE始终优于最近的隐私保护MoE统一方法。

Conclusion: MetaMoE成功实现了在隐私约束下将分布式专家统一为单一MoE模型，为联邦学习和隐私保护场景下的模型扩展提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale capacity by combining specialized experts, but most existing approaches assume centralized access to training data. In practice, data are distributed across clients and cannot be shared due to privacy constraints, making unified MoE training challenging. We propose MetaMoE, a privacy-preserving framework that unifies independently trained, domain-specialized experts into a single MoE using public proxy data as surrogates for inaccessible private data. Central to MetaMoE is diversity-aware proxy selection, which selects client-domain-relevant and diverse samples from public data to effectively approximate private data distributions and supervise router learning. These proxies are further used to align expert training, improving expert coordination at unification time, while a context-aware router enhances expert selection across heterogeneous inputs. Experiments on computer vision and natural language processing benchmarks demonstrate that MetaMoE consistently outperforms recent privacy-preserving MoE unification methods. Code is available at https://github.com/ws-jiang/MetaMoE.

</details>


### [168] [Minimal-Intervention KV Retention: A Design-Space Study and a Diversity-Penalty Survivor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14292)
*Libo Sun,Po-wei Harn,Peixiong He,Xiao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在低预算KV缓存压缩场景下，简单的评分函数修改（α方法）击败了复杂的结构重设计，通过严格的预注册实验协议得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低预算KV缓存压缩是一个拥挤的设计空间，涉及缓存表示、注意力头路由、压缩频率、解码行为和预算内评分等多个维度。作者旨在研究这一领域中哪种方法最有效。

Method: 1. 在五个设计家族中研究七种机制，在匹配的平均缓存条件下进行实验；2. 提出α方法：对TriAttention保留评分器进行单函数修改，用基于设施位置的贪婪选择替换argmax-top-k，引入V空间冗余惩罚（由权重λ控制）；3. 采用预注册协议，在冻结的开发集上调优λ，并在独立的留出集上确认。

Result: 七种现有机制全部被拒绝；α方法在λ=0.5时，在四个（模型，预算）组合中的两个（Qwen b=128和Llama b=64）通过Bonferroni校正检验，无显著负结果，触发了预注册的Branch A。

Conclusion: 发现具有不对称性：在低预算场景下，最小化的评分修改击败了更重的结构重设计。这种不对称性的发现得益于严格的匹配内存、sympy评分、留出确认协议所提供的证据标准。

Abstract: KV-cache compression at small budgets is a crowded design space spanning cache representation, head-wise routing, compression cadence, decoding behavior, and within-budget scoring. We study seven mechanisms across these five families under matched mean cache on long-form mathematical reasoning (MATH-500~\cite{hendrycks2021math}) with two distilled-reasoning models (Qwen-7B and Llama-8B variants of DeepSeek-R1-Distill~\cite{deepseek2025r1}) at budgets $b \in \{64, 128\}$. All seven were rejected. We then propose $α$, a one-function modification to the TriAttention~\cite{mao2026triattention} retention scorer that replaces argmax-top-$k$ with greedy facility-location-inspired selection under a V-space redundancy penalty controlled by a single weight $λ$. A pre-registered protocol tunes $λ$ on a frozen development split and confirms on a disjoint held-out split; with $λ= 0.5$, $α$ clears Bonferroni on two of the four (model, budget) cells (Qwen $b{=}128$ and Llama $b{=}64$), no cell is significantly negative, and the pre-registered Branch~A triggers. The finding is asymmetric: a minimal scoring modification beat heavier structural redesigns in this regime, and the combined matched-memory, sympy-graded, held-out confirmation protocol is the evidence standard that made the asymmetry visible.

</details>


### [169] [Policy Optimization in Hybrid Discrete-Continuous Action Spaces via Mixed Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14297)
*Matias Alvo,Daniel Russo,Yash Kanoria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了混合策略优化(HPO)算法，通过结合路径梯度和分数函数梯度，在混合离散-连续动作空间中有效解决了标准策略梯度方法的信用分配问题，在库存控制和切换线性二次调节器问题上显著优于PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合离散-连续动作空间（如机器人、控制和运营问题）中，标准的无模型策略梯度方法依赖分数函数估计器，在高维设置下存在严重的信用分配问题，导致梯度质量差；而可微分仿真虽然可以通过反向传播避免这些问题，但离散动作或非光滑动态会导致梯度有偏或信息量不足。

Method: 提出混合策略优化(HPO)算法，在光滑性允许的情况下通过仿真器反向传播，使用混合梯度估计器将路径梯度和分数函数梯度结合，同时保持无偏性；同时展示了如何将具有动作不连续性的问题重新表述为混合形式。

Result: 在库存控制和切换线性二次调节器问题上，HPO显著优于PPO，且随着连续动作维度的增加，性能差距扩大；理论分析了混合梯度的结构，证明其交叉项（捕获连续动作如何影响未来离散决策）在离散最优响应附近可忽略，从而实现连续和离散分量的近似分散更新，并在最优性附近降低方差。

Conclusion: HPO算法成功解决了混合离散-连续动作空间中的强化学习挑战，通过混合梯度估计器有效结合了两种梯度方法的优势，为机器人、控制和运营优化等领域的混合动作空间问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning in hybrid discrete-continuous action spaces, such as settings where the discrete component selects a regime (or index) and the continuous component optimizes within it -- a structure common in robotics, control, and operations problems. Standard model-free policy gradient methods rely on score-function (SF) estimators and suffer from severe credit-assignment issues in high-dimensional settings, leading to poor gradient quality. On the other hand, differentiable simulation largely sidesteps these issues by backpropagating through a simulator, but the presence of discrete actions or non-smooth dynamics yields biased or uninformative gradients. To address this, we propose Hybrid Policy Optimization (HPO), which backpropagates through the simulator wherever smoothness permits, using a mixed gradient estimator that combines pathwise and SF gradients while maintaining unbiasedness. We also show how problems with action discontinuities can be reformulated in hybrid form, further broadening its applicability. Empirically, HPO substantially outperforms PPO on inventory control and switched linear-quadratic regulator problems, with performance gaps increasing as the continuous action dimension grows. Finally, we characterize the structure of the mixed gradient, showing that its cross term -- which captures how continuous actions influence future discrete decisions -- becomes negligible near a discrete best response, thereby enabling approximate decentralized updates of the continuous and discrete components and reducing variance near optimality. All resources are available at github.com/MatiasAlvo/hybrid-rl.

</details>


### [170] [Language-Induced Priors for Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14301)
*Qiyuan Chen,Jiayu Zhou,Raed Al Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了语言诱导先验（LIP）框架，利用预训练大语言模型从目标域的文本描述中学习先验，解决域适应中目标数据稀缺时的负迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域适应在冷启动场景下存在根本性悖论：当目标域数据稀缺时，统计方法无法有效区分相关和不相关的源域，常导致负迁移现象。虽然目标域的专家文本描述通常是可获得的，但现有研究忽视了这一重要资源。

Method: 提出概率框架将语义描述转化为选择模型（LIP），通过预训练大语言模型学习偏好；将LIP集成到期望最大化算法中以识别源域相关性。该框架兼容任何具有似然函数的参数模型，允许LIP在目标信号微弱时引导源域选择，并随样本积累逐步优化选择。

Result: 理论上证明了估计器在正确先验下近似达到预言机冷启动均方误差（MSE），且无论LIP质量如何均保持渐近一致性。实验验证涵盖描述性任务（高斯估计）、预测性任务（C-MAPSS数据集）和规范性任务（MuJoCo hopper）。

Conclusion: 该框架成功利用文本描述引导域适应，在冷启动场景下有效避免了负迁移，同时随着数据积累能够渐进修正先验偏差，为小样本域适应提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Domain adaptation faces a fundamental paradox in the cold-start regime. When target data is scarce, statistical methods fail to distinguish relevant source domains from irrelevant ones, which often leads to negative transfer. In this paper, we address this challenge by leveraging expert textual descriptions of the target domain, a resource that is often available but overlooked. We propose a probabilistic framework that translates these semantic descriptions into a choice model, namely a Language-Induced Prior (LIP), that learns the preferences from a pretrained Large Language Model (LLM). The LIP is then integrated into an Expectation-Maximization algorithm to identify source relevance. Methodologically, this framework is compatible with any parametric model where a likelihood is available. It allows the LIP to guide the selection of sources when target signals are weak, while gradually refining these choices as samples accumulate. Theoretically, we prove that the estimator roughly matches an oracle cold-start MSE under a correct prior, while remaining asymptotically consistent regardless of the quality of the LIP. Empirically, we validated the framework on a descriptive (Gaussian estimation), a predictive (C-MAPSS dataset), and a prescriptive task (MuJoCo hopper).

</details>


### [171] [Matrix-Space Reinforcement Learning for Reusing Local Transition Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14304)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Carlee Joe-Wong,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出矩阵空间强化学习（MSRL），利用半正定矩阵描述子捕获轨迹段的低阶统计特性，实现跨任务的几何抽象与组合泛化，显著提升了新任务的学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在序列决策的组合泛化中常忽略丰富的局部转移几何与动力学信息，难以有效识别和迁移先前经验中对新任务有用的部分，限制了迁移学习的性能。

Method: 提出MSRL框架，用半正定矩阵描述子对轨迹段的一步转移的一阶和二阶统计量进行聚合，形成几何抽象表示。该描述子支持代数组合，证明其在坐标规范下良定义、对诱导低阶可加信号类完备、可加且在可容许描述子中最小充分。将价值函数以轨迹段矩阵为条件，得到动作价值的一阶平滑近似，实现源任务学习到的矩阵-价值映射对新任务的引导。该方法可与现有无模型和基于模型方法即插即用，并通过阻碍过滤剔除不合理的组合。

Result: 理论证明了描述子的良定义性、完备性、可加性和最小充分性；实证表明MSRL在有限预算下的目标AUC平均值达到0.73，优于从零开始训练的MSRL（0.65）、TD-MPC-PT+FT（0.63）和TD-MPC（0.57）。

Conclusion: MSRL通过几何抽象有效捕获轨迹段的结构信息，实现了跨任务的知识迁移与组合泛化，在性能上优于现有主流方法，为序列决策中的迁移学习提供了新的理论工具和实践范式。

Abstract: Compositional generalization in sequential decision-making requires identifying which parts of prior rollouts remain useful for new tasks. Existing methods reuse skills or predictive models, but often overlook rich local transition geometry and dynamics. We propose Matrix-Space Reinforcement Learning (MSRL), a geometric abstraction that represents trajectory segments through positive semidefinite matrix descriptors aggregating first- and second-order statistics of lifted one-step transitions. These descriptors expose shared hidden structure, support algebraic composition in an abstract matrix space, and reveal opportunities for transfer. We prove that the descriptor is well defined up to coordinate gauge, complete for the induced low-order additive signal class, additive under valid segment composition, and minimally sufficient among admissible additive descriptors. We further show that conditioning value functions on the trajectory-segment matrix yields a first-order smooth approximation of action values, enabling source-learned matrix-to-value mappings to bootstrap learning in new tasks. MSRL is plug-in compatible with standard model-free and model-based methods, while obstruction filtering rejects implausible compositions. Empirically, MSRL achieves the best average finite-budget target AUC of 0.73, outperforming MSRL from scratch (0.65), TD-MPC-PT+FT (0.63), and TD-MPC (0.57).

</details>


### [172] [Beyond Binary: Reframing GUI Critique as Continuous Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14311)
*Yuchen Sun,Pei Fu,Shaojie Zhang,Anan Du,Xiuwen Xi,Ruoceng Zhang,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan,Chongyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出测试时扩展（TTS）中现有GUI评论模型采用二分类存在严重缺陷，提出BBCritic通过两阶段对比学习在共享Affordance空间中对齐指令与动作，恢复层级结构；构建了首个具有四级分层标注的GUI评论基准BBBench；实验表明BBCritic-3B无需额外标注即超越7B参数的二分类SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI评论模型统一采用二分类范式，导致细粒度排序能力不足。动机分析揭示了严重的纠缠问题：有效动作与看似合理但无效的干扰项的分数变得不可区分。作者将此失败归因于两个结构性缺陷：（1）Affordance Collapse——层级化的可供性空间被压缩为0/1标签；（2）Noise Sensitivity——二分类目标过拟合于噪声决策边界。

Method: 提出BBCritic（Beyond-Binary Critic），基于功能等价假说进行范式转换。通过两阶段对比学习，BBCritic在共享的Affordance空间中对齐指令与动作，恢复被二分类监督所扁平化的层级结构。同时构建BBBench——首个GUI评论基准，其将密集动作空间与分层四级分类法配对，支持细粒度排序评估。

Result: BBCritic-3B在无额外标注训练的情况下，性能超越7B参数的二分类SOTA模型。在跨平台和跨任务场景中展现出强大的零样本迁移能力。实验结果支持了作者的方法论观点：GUI评论本质上是一个度量学习问题，而非分类问题。

Conclusion: GUI评论应当被视为度量学习问题而非分类问题。通过超越二分类范式并在Affordance空间中恢复层级结构，BBCritic有效解决了现有方法的纠缠问题，为通用GUI智能体的测试时扩展提供了更可靠的评论机制。

Abstract: Test-Time Scaling (TTS), which samples multiple candidate actions and ranks them via a Critic Model, has emerged as a promising paradigm for generalist GUI agents. Its efficacy thus hinges on the critic's fine-grained ranking ability. However, existing GUI critic models uniformly adopt binary classification. Our motivational analysis of these models exposes a severe entanglement: scores for valid actions and plausible-but-invalid distractors become indistinguishable. We attribute this failure to two structural defects: Affordance Collapse--the hierarchical affordance space is compressed into 0/1 labels; and Noise Sensitivity--binary objectives overfit to noisy decision boundaries. To resolve this, we introduce BBCritic (Beyond-Binary Critic), a paradigm shift grounded in the Functional Equivalence Hypothesis. Through two-stage contrastive learning, BBCritic aligns instructions and actions in a shared Affordance Space, recovering the hierarchical structure that binary supervision flattens. We also present BBBench (Beyond-Binary Bench), the first GUI critic benchmark that pairs a dense action space with a hierarchical four-level taxonomy, enabling fine-grained ranking evaluation. Experimental results show that BBCritic-3B, trained without any extra annotation, outperforms 7B-parameter SOTA binary models. It demonstrates strong zero-shot transferability across platforms and tasks, supporting our methodological view: GUI critique is fundamentally a metric-learning problem, not a classification one.

</details>


### [173] [Guided Diffusion Sampling for Precipitation Forecast Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14317)
*Ayumu Ueyama,Kazuhiko Kawamoto,Hiroshi Kera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次探索了利用基于扩散模型的数据驱动天气预报模型进行降水削减干预。提出了一种基于梯度的引导框架，通过引导扩散采样轨迹而非直接扰动大气状态，在实现有效降水削减的同时，保持了与大气分布的一致性，并证明了该方法比对抗性扰动具有更高的物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端降水造成严重的社会和经济损失，天气控制被视为潜在的缓解策略。然而，目前尚未有研究探索在数据驱动天气预报模型中使用基于扰动的干预措施。虽然对抗性攻击也能产生改变预测的扰动，但其旨在利用模型伪影，并未考虑物理合理性，因此亟需一种既能改变天气预测又符合物理规律的方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于梯度的引导框架，用于基于扩散的天气预报模型中的降水削减干预。该方法不直接扰动大气状态，而是通过梯度引导来调整扩散采样轨迹，从而在保持与大气分布一致性的前提下实现降水削减。为了评估干预的物理合理性，从三个维度进行了分析：(i) 垂直和变量维度的扰动剖面；(ii) 潜空间轨迹偏差；(iii) 跨模型迁移性。

Result: 在WeatherBench2的极端降水事件实验中，该方法成功实现了有效的降水削减。评估结果表明，相较于对抗性扰动，本文方法产生的干预在垂直剖面、潜空间轨迹和跨模型迁移性方面均表现出更高的物理合理性。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了通过引导扩散采样轨迹进行天气控制干预的可行性。该方法不仅能够有效减少极端降水预测，而且比传统的对抗性攻击更能保证干预措施的物理合理性，为基于数据驱动模型的天气控制提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Extreme precipitation causes severe societal and economic damage, and weather control has long been discussed as a potential mitigation strategy. However, to the best of our knowledge, perturbation-based interventions for weather control using data-driven weather forecasting models have not yet been explored. While adversarial attacks also generate perturbations that alter forecasts, they aim to exploit model artifacts and do not account for physical plausibility. In this paper, we propose a gradient-based guidance framework for precipitation-reduction interventions through diffusion sampling in diffusion-based weather forecasting models. Instead of directly perturbing atmospheric states, our method steers the diffusion sampling trajectory, enabling precipitation reduction while maintaining consistency with the atmospheric distribution. To assess physical plausibility, we evaluate from three perspectives: (i) vertical and variable-wise perturbation profiles, (ii) latent-space trajectory deviation, and (iii) cross-model transferability. Experiments on extreme precipitation events from WeatherBench2 demonstrate that our method achieves effective precipitation reduction while yielding more physically plausible interventions than adversarial perturbations.

</details>


### [174] [Dynamic Latent Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14323)
*Fangyuan Yu,Xin Su,Amir Abdullah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出广义Dijkstra搜索证明MDP中子策略时序组合的全局最优性，并基于此提出动态潜在路由(DLR)方法，在低数据微调设置下相比监督微调平均提升6.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究马尔可夫决策过程中时变奖励函数下子策略的时序连接问题，探索如何通过中间最优子策略的时序组合恢复全局最优的目标达成策略。

Method: 提出广义Dijkstra搜索(GDS)算法并证明其理论性质；基于"搜索、选择、更新"原则，设计动态潜在路由(DLR)语言模型后训练方法，在单一训练阶段联合学习离散潜在编码、路由策略和模型参数。

Result: 在低数据微调设置下，DLR在四个数据集和六个模型上匹配或超越监督微调(SFT)，平均提升+6.6个百分点；先前的离散潜在基线方法均一致性地表现不如SFT。机制分析和代码消融实验表明DLR学习到了具有不同因果角色的结构化路由行为。

Conclusion: 通过理论证明和实验验证，展示了基于动态搜索的策略组合方法在语言模型微调中的有效性，为离散潜在变量方法在低数据场景下提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We investigate the temporal concatenation of sub-policies in Markov Decision Processes (MDP) with time-varying reward functions. We introduce General Dijkstra Search (GDS), and prove that globally optimal goal-reaching policies can be recovered through temporal composition of intermediate optimal sub-policies. Motivated by the "search, select, update" principle underlying GDS, we propose Dynamic Latent Routing (DLR), a language-model post-training method that jointly learns discrete latent codes, routing policies, and model parameters through dynamic search in a single training stage. In low-data fine-tuning settings, DLR matches or outperforms supervised fine-tuning across four datasets and six models, achieving a mean gain of +6.6 percentage points, while prior discrete-latent baselines consistently underperform SFT. Mechanistic analyses and targeted code ablations show that DLR learns structured routing behaviors with distinct causal roles.

</details>


### [175] [AIM-DDI: A Model-Agnostic Multimodal Integration Module for Drug-Drug Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14327)
*Yerin Park,Sangseon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AIM-DDI，一个架构无关的多模态融合模块，通过将异构模态信息表示为共享潜在空间中的tokens，实现了跨不同DDI预测架构的模型无关集成，在最具挑战性的双药物未见设置下显著提升了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物-药物相互作用（DDI）预测是计算生物医学中的关键任务，因为联合用药的不良相互作用可能导致严重的副作用和临床风险。现有挑战在于未见药物泛化问题，即需要对训练中未观察到的药物进行相互作用预测。虽然多模态DDI模型利用了多样化的药物相关信息，但其融合机制通常与特定的预测架构绑定，限制了其在不同模型间的复用。

Method: 提出AIM-DDI（架构无关多模态集成模块），将异构模态信息表示为共享潜在空间中的tokens。通过统一的融合模块建模跨模态tokens的依赖关系，实现对结构、化学和语义药物信号的模型无关集成，可应用于不同的DDI预测架构。

Result: 在多种DDI模型和基于DrugBank的设置上进行了广泛评估，结果表明AIM-DDI持续改善预测性能，在最具挑战性的双未见设置（测试药物对中的两种药物在训练期间均未被观察到）下取得最强的性能提升。

Conclusion: 将多模态集成视为可复用模块而非模型特定的融合组件，是鲁棒的未见药物DDI预测的有效策略，为解决DDI预测中的未见药物泛化问题提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Drug-drug interaction (DDI) prediction is a critical task in computational biomedicine, as adverse interactions between co-administered drugs can cause severe side effects and clinical risks. A key challenge is unseen-drug generalization, where interactions must be predicted for drugs not observed during training. Although multimodal DDI models exploit diverse drug-related information, their fusion mechanisms are often tied to specific prediction architectures, limiting their reuse across models. To address this, we propose AIM-DDI, an architecture-independent multimodal integration module that represents heterogeneous modality information as tokens in a shared latent space. By modeling dependencies across modality tokens through a unified fusion module, AIM-DDI enables model-agnostic integration of structural, chemical, and semantic drug signals across different DDI prediction architectures. Extensive evaluations across diverse DDI models and DrugBank-based settings show that AIM-DDI consistently improves prediction performance, with the strongest gains under the most challenging both-unseen setting where neither drug in a test pair is observed during training. These results suggest that treating multimodal integration as a reusable module, rather than a model-specific fusion component, is an effective strategy for robust unseen-drug DDI prediction.

</details>


### [176] [Nearest-Neighbor Radii under Dependent Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14343)
*Yuanyuan Gao,Yilong Hou,Zhexiao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了强混合依赖观测下的最近邻半径性质，证明了在多项式混合条件下分布无关的几乎必然收敛，以及在几何混合条件下依赖于局部内在维度的精确非渐近矩界，为依赖采样下的最近邻方法提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近邻方法是经典与现代机器学习的基础技术，但其几何性质通常在独立采样假设下进行分析。实际应用中数据往往存在依赖性（如时间序列），因此需要研究依赖采样是否改变了最近邻邻域的尺度性质。

Method: 考虑强混合（strong mixing）依赖观测，在多项式混合条件下建立分布无关的几乎必然收敛理论，在几何混合条件下推导精确的非渐近矩界。矩界依赖于局部内在维度而非环境维度，使其适用于集中在低维流形附近的高维数据。

Result: 在多项式混合下证明了最近邻半径的分布无关几乎必然收敛；在几何混合下获得了依赖于局部内在维度的精确非渐近矩界。合成实验和真实世界时间序列基准测试支持了理论结果，表明最近邻几何在依赖采样下仍然具有信息性。

Conclusion: 依赖采样不改变最近邻邻域的基本尺度性质，最近邻几何在依赖观测下仍然保持其信息价值，这为时间序列等依赖数据上的最近邻方法应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor methods are fundamental to classical and modern machine learning, yet their geometric properties are typically analyzed under independent sampling. In this paper, we study the nearest-neighbor radii under dependent sampling. We consider strong mixing dependent observations and ask whether dependence changes the scale of nearest-neighbor neighborhoods. We establish distribution-free almost sure convergence under polynomial mixing and sharp non-asymptotic moment bounds under geometric mixing. The moment bounds depend on the local intrinsic dimension rather than the ambient dimension, making the results applicable to high-dimensional data concentrated near lower-dimensional manifolds. Synthetic experiments and real-world time-series benchmarks support the theory, showing that nearest-neighbor geometry remains informative under dependence sampling.

</details>


### [177] [Exemplar Partitioning for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14347)
*Jessica Rumbelow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Exemplar Partitioning (EP)，一种无监督方法，能用比稀疏自编码器(SAE)少约1000倍的token构建可解释的特征字典，在Gemma-2-2B上实现了更优的潜在概念检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏自编码器(SAE)方法需要大量训练数据和计算资源来构建特征字典，且不同模型/层之间的字典难以直接比较。作者希望开发一种更高效、更易比较的激活空间分解方法。

Method: EP通过对激活空间进行Voronoi划分构建特征字典：使用leader-clustering算法在距离阈值内对流式激活进行聚类，每个Voronoi区域由一个观测到的样例锚定，该样例既作为成员标准也作为干预方向。字典大小不由预设决定，而是由该阈值下的激活几何结构确定。由于样例是观测而非学习的，同一数据流构建的字典可直接跨层、跨模型、跨训练检查点比较。

Result: 在Gemma-2-2B上的实验表明：1)EP字典区域具有可解释性并支持因果干预；2)跨检查点匹配可区分微调保留和引入的方向；3)约20%的EP区域与SAE特征匹配(F1>0.5)，EP单热探针保留了原始激活探针约97%的准确率；4)在AxBench潜在概念检测任务上，EP达到AUROC 0.881，比GemmaScope SAE高0.126，仅比SAE-A低0.030，但构建计算量减少约1000倍。

Conclusion: EP是一种高效且有效的可解释性工具，能以极低的计算成本实现与SAE相当或更优的性能，并提供了跨模型比较、OOD检测等新能力，为LLM可解释性研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: We introduce Exemplar Partitioning (EP), an unsupervised method for constructing interpretable feature dictionaries from large language model activations with $\sim 10^{3}\times$ fewer tokens than comparable sparse autoencoders (SAEs). An EP dictionary is a Voronoi partition of activation space, built by leader-clustering streamed activations within a distance threshold. Each region is anchored by an observed exemplar that serves as both its membership criterion and intervention direction; dictionary size is not prespecified, but determined by the activation geometry at that threshold. Because exemplars are observed rather than learned, dictionaries built from the same data stream are directly comparable across layers, models, and training checkpoints.
  We characterise EP as an interpretability object via targeted demonstrations of properties newly accessible through this construction, plus one head-to-head benchmark. In Gemma-2-2B, EP dictionary regions are interpretable and support causal interventions: refusal in instruction-tuned Gemma concentrates in a region whose exemplar ablation can collapse held-out refusal. Cross-checkpoint matching between base and instruction-tuned dictionaries separates the directions preserved through finetuning from those introduced by it. EP regions and Gemma Scope SAE features decompose activation space differently but agree on a shared core: $\sim 20\%$ of EP regions match an SAE feature at $F_{1} > 0.5$, and EP one-hot probes retain $\sim 97\%$ of raw-activation probe accuracy at $\ell_{0} = 1$. Nearest-exemplar distance provides a free out-of-distribution signal at inference. On AxBench latent concept detection at Gemma-2-2B-it L20, EP at $p_{1}$ reaches mean AUROC $0.881$, $+0.126$ over the canonical GemmaScope SAE leaderboard entry and within $0.030$ of SAE-A's $0.911$, at $\sim 10^{3}\times$ less build compute.

</details>


### [178] [Distributionally Robust Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning via Adaptive Task Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14350)
*Nicholas E. Corrado,Wenyuan Huang,Josiah P. Hanna*

Main category: cs.LG
